independent of
T,\delta_{0}
such that for any
z^{[i]}\in\overline{\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}}
,
s>s_{1}\geq s_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}H_{5}\left(z^{[i]},s,w,\sigma\right)\left|\tilde{g}_{1,i}(w,\sigma)\right|dwd\sigma+\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}H_{5}\left(z^{[i]},s,(\tilde{w},0),\sigma\right)\left|g_{2,i}(\tilde{w},\sigma)\right|d\tilde{w}d\sigma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\Big[R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}+R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-(l_{i}+\frac{3}{8})s_{1}}+\min\Big\{e^{-s_{1}},e^{-s_{1}}e^{-(l_{i}+1)s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\min\Big\{e^{-\frac{s_{1}}{2}},e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}|\tilde{z}^{[i]}|^{\frac{10}{3}l_{i}+\frac{10}{3}}\Big\}\Big].

Proof.

By (
6.12
), (
6.9
), (
6.21
), Lemma
6.1
,

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{g}_{1,i}(z^{[i]},s)\right|\lesssim e^{-s}\left|\mathcal{G}_{1,i}\right|+se^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}\mathbf{1}_{|z^{[i]}|\leq 2C_{\tilde{e}}}\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mu_{i}^{-2}e^{-(l_{i}+1)s}\langle y^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{9}{4}}+se^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}+e^{-s}\mathbf{1}_{\delta e^{\frac{s}{2}}\leq|z^{[i]}|\leq 2\delta e^{\frac{s}{2}}},

\displaystyle\left|g_{2,i}(\tilde{z}^{[i]},s)\right|\lesssim e^{-\frac{s}{2}}\left|\mathcal{G}_{2,i}\right|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mu_{i}^{-1}e^{-(l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{7}{4}}+e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}+e^{-\frac{s}{2}}\min\Big\{1,e^{-\frac{5}{3}l_{i}s}|\tilde{z}^{[i]}|^{\frac{10}{3}l_{i}+\frac{10}{3}}\Big\},

where we used

\delta_{0}\in(0,1)
.
By (
5.5
), (
6.18
),

R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mu_{i}^{-2}e^{-(l_{i}+1)s}\langle y^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{9}{4}}\sim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{(3l_{i}+3)s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{9}{4}},\quad R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mu_{i}^{-1}e^{-(l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{7}{4}}\sim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{(l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}\tilde{z}^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{7}{4}}.

By Lemma
2.10
(
1
),

\displaystyle\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}H_{5}\left(z^{[i]},s,w,\sigma\right)R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{(3l_{i}+3)\sigma}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})\sigma}w\rangle^{-\frac{9}{4}}dwd\sigma

\displaystyle+\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}H_{5}\left(z^{[i]},s,(\tilde{w},0),\sigma\right)R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{(l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})\sigma}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})\sigma}\tilde{w}\rangle^{-\frac{7}{4}}d\tilde{w}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left[e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}+e^{-(\frac{3}{2}l_{i}+\frac{3}{8})s_{1}}+e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{3}{4}}+e^{-(\frac{5}{2}l_{i}+\frac{9}{8})s_{1}}\right]

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left[e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}+e^{-(\frac{3}{2}l_{i}+\frac{3}{8})s_{1}}\right].

By Lemma
2.10
(
3
), for an arbitrarily small constant
\epsilon\in(0,1/4)
,

\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}H_{5}\left(z^{[i]},s,w,\sigma\right)\sigma e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})\sigma}dwd\sigma\lesssim_{\epsilon}\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}H_{5}\left(z^{[i]},s,w,\sigma\right)e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon)\sigma}dwd\sigma\lesssim_{\epsilon}e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon)s_{1}}.

By Lemma
2.10
(
3
) (
2
),

\displaystyle\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}H_{5}\left(z^{[i]},s,w,\sigma\right)\left(e^{-\sigma}\mathbf{1}_{\delta e^{\frac{\sigma}{2}}\leq|w|\leq 2\delta e^{\frac{\sigma}{2}}}\right)dwd\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}H_{5}\left(z^{[i]},s,w,\sigma\right)\min\Big\{e^{-\sigma},e^{-(l_{i}+2)\sigma}|w|^{2l_{i}+2}\Big\}dwd\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\min\Big\{e^{-s_{1}},\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\left[e^{-\sigma}e^{-(l_{i}+1)s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}+e^{-(l_{i}+2)\sigma}\right]d\sigma\Big\}\lesssim\min\Big\{e^{-s_{1}},e^{-s_{1}}e^{-(l_{i}+1)s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}+e^{-(l_{i}+2)s_{1}}\Big\}.

By Lemma
2.10
(
4
),

\displaystyle\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}H_{5}\left(z^{[i]},s,(\tilde{w},0),\sigma\right)\left[e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})\sigma}+\min\Big\{e^{-\frac{\sigma}{2}},e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})\sigma}|\tilde{w}|^{\frac{10}{3}l_{i}+\frac{10}{3}}\Big\}\right]d\tilde{w}d\sigma

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\min\Big\{e^{-\frac{s_{1}}{2}},e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}|\tilde{z}^{[i]}|^{\frac{10}{3}l_{i}+\frac{10}{3}}+e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s_{1}}\Big\}.

Combining the above estimates and using
C_{1}+\min\{C_{2},C_{3}\}=\min\{C_{1}+C_{2},C_{1}+C_{3}\}
, then we get the desired estimate. ∎

Proposition 6.5
.

Under all assumptions in Lemma
6.1
, then there exists a constant
C>0
independent of
T,\delta_{0}
such that for any
z^{[i]}\in\overline{\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}}
,
s>s_{0}
, we have

(6.25)

\displaystyle\left|\Phi_{i}(z^{[i]},s)\right|\leq

\displaystyle C\Big[\Big(R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}+T^{\frac{3}{8}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}}\rangle+e^{-(l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{|z^{[i]}|\leq e^{\frac{l_{i}s}{2l_{i}+2}}}

\displaystyle+R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mathbf{1}_{|z^{[i]}|>e^{\frac{l_{i}s}{2l_{i}+2}}}\Big].

Proof.

Applying Lemma
6.4
with
s_{1}=s_{0}
, we get
|\Phi_{i}|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}
in
\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}\times(s_{0},\infty)
.
For the more delicate estimate, we separate the domain into the following four parts and estimate separately.

Case 1: Fix
z^{[i]}\in\overline{\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}}
and
s\in(s_{0},s_{0}+2]
.
By Lemma
6.4
with
s_{1}=s_{0}
,

\big|\Phi_{i}(z^{[i]},s)\big|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}+R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-(l_{i}+\frac{3}{8})s_{0}}+e^{-s_{0}}e^{-(l_{i}+1)s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}+e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}|z^{[i]}|^{\frac{10}{3}l_{i}+\frac{10}{3}}.

Case 2: Fix
|z^{[i]}|\leq 2
and
s\in(s_{0}+2,\infty)
.

By uniqueness, we split
\Phi_{i}=\Phi_{i}^{(1)}+\Phi_{i}^{(2)}
and rewrite (
6.11
) as

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{s_{*}}\Phi_{i}^{(1)}=A_{w}\Phi_{i}^{(1)}+\tilde{g}_{1,i}(w,s_{*})\mbox{ \ for \ }(w,s_{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\times(s-1,\infty),\\
&-\partial_{w_{5}}\Phi_{i}^{(1)}((\tilde{w},0),s_{*})=g_{2,i}(\tilde{w},s_{*})\mbox{ \ for \ }(\tilde{w},s_{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{4}\times(s-1,\infty),\quad\Phi_{i}^{(1)}(\cdot,s-1)=0\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+},\end{aligned}\right.

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{s_{*}}\Phi_{i}^{(2)}=A_{w}\Phi_{i}^{(2)}\mbox{ \ for \ }(w,s_{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\times(s-1,\infty),\\
&-\partial_{w_{5}}\Phi_{i}^{(2)}((\tilde{w},0),s_{*})=0\mbox{ \ for \ }(\tilde{w},s_{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{4}\times(s-1,\infty),\quad\Phi_{i}^{(2)}(\cdot,s-1)=\Phi_{i}(\cdot,s-1)\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}.\end{aligned}\right.

For
\Phi_{i}^{(1)}
, applying Lemma
6.4
with
s_{1}=s-1
at the point
(z^{[i]},s)
, we have

\big|\Phi_{i}^{(1)}(z^{[i]},s)\big|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}+R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-(l_{i}+\frac{3}{8})s}+e^{-s}e^{-(l_{i}+1)s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}+e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}|z^{[i]}|^{\frac{10}{3}l_{i}+\frac{10}{3}}.

For
\Phi_{i}^{(2)}
, by Lemma
2.10
(
2
) with
s_{1}=s-1
,
|z^{[i]}|\leq 2
, and Lemma
6.3
, then

\big|\Phi_{i}^{(2)}(z^{[i]},s)\big|\lesssim\|\Phi_{i}(\cdot,s-1)\|_{L_{\rho}^{2}(\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5})}\lesssim se^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}.

Since
se^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-(l_{i}+\frac{3}{8})s}
, we have

\left|\Phi_{i}(z^{[i]},s)\right|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}+R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-(l_{i}+\frac{3}{8})s}+e^{-s}e^{-(l_{i}+1)s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}+e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}|z^{[i]}|^{\frac{10}{3}l_{i}+\frac{10}{3}}.

Case 3: Fix
2<|z^{[i]}|<e^{\frac{s-s_{0}}{2}}
and
s\in(s_{0}+2,\infty)
.

We choose
s_{1}\in(s_{0},s-1)
such that
|z^{[i]}|=e^{\frac{s-s_{1}}{2}}
.
Similar to Case 2, we split
\Phi_{i}=\Phi_{i}^{(3)}+\Phi_{i}^{(4)}
and the equation (
6.11
) is rewritten as

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{s_{*}}\Phi_{i}^{(3)}=A_{w}\Phi_{i}^{(3)}+\tilde{g}_{1,i}(w,s_{*})\mbox{ \ for \ }(w,s_{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\times(s_{1},\infty),\\
&-\partial_{w_{5}}\Phi_{i}^{(3)}((\tilde{w},0),s_{*})=g_{2,i}(\tilde{w},s_{*})\mbox{ \ for \ }(\tilde{w},s_{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{4}\times(s_{1},\infty),\quad\Phi_{i}^{(3)}(\cdot,s_{1})=0\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+},\end{aligned}\right.

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{s_{*}}\Phi_{i}^{(4)}=A_{w}\Phi_{i}^{(4)}\mbox{ \ for \ }(w,s_{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\times(s_{1},\infty),\\
&-\partial_{w_{5}}\Phi_{i}^{(4)}((\tilde{w},0),s_{*})=0\mbox{ \ for \ }(\tilde{w},s_{*})\in\mathbb{R}^{4}\times(s_{1},\infty),\quad\Phi_{i}^{(4)}(\cdot,s_{1})=\Phi_{i}(\cdot,s_{1})\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}.\end{aligned}\right.

By Lemma
6.4
, we have

\big|\Phi_{i}^{(3)}(z^{[i]},s)\big|\lesssim T^{\frac{3}{8}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}}+Te^{-(l_{i}+1)s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}+e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}|z^{[i]}|^{\frac{10}{3}l_{i}+\frac{10}{3}},

where we used

\displaystyle e^{-s_{1}}=e^{-s}|z^{[i]}|^{2},\quad R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s_{1}}e^{-(\frac{l_{i}}{2}+\frac{3}{8})s}|z^{[i]}|^{-\frac{1}{4}}\lesssim T^{\frac{3}{8}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}},

\displaystyle R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-(l_{i}+\frac{3}{8})s_{1}}=e^{-\frac{3}{8}s_{1}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}}\leq T^{\frac{3}{8}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}}.

By Lemma
2.10
(
2
),
|z^{[i]}|=e^{\frac{s-s_{1}}{2}}
,
s_{1}\leq s-1
, and Lemma
6.3
, we obtain that

\big|\Phi_{i}^{(4)}(z^{[i]},s)\big|\lesssim\|\Phi_{i}(\cdot,s_{1})\|_{L_{\rho}^{2}(\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5})}\lesssim s_{1}e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s_{1}}\lesssim(-\ln T)T^{\frac{1}{2}}e^{-l_{i}s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}}.

Since
(-\ln T)T^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim T^{\frac{3}{8}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}
, we have

\big|\Phi_{i}(z^{[i]},s)\big|\lesssim T^{\frac{3}{8}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}}+Te^{-(l_{i}+1)s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}+e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}|z^{[i]}|^{\frac{10}{3}l_{i}+\frac{10}{3}}.

Case 4: Fix
|z^{[i]}|\geq e^{\frac{s-s_{0}}{2}}
and
s\in(s_{0}+2,\infty)
.
By Lemma
6.4
with
s_{1}=s_{0}
,

\left|\Phi_{i}(z^{[i]},s)\right|\lesssim T^{\frac{3}{8}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}}+Te^{-(l_{i}+1)s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}+e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}|z^{[i]}|^{\frac{10}{3}l_{i}+\frac{10}{3}},

where we used

\displaystyle e^{-s_{0}}\leq e^{-s}|z^{[i]}|^{2},\quad R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s_{0}}e^{-(\frac{l_{i}}{2}+\frac{3}{8})s}|z^{[i]}|^{-\frac{1}{4}}\lesssim T^{\frac{3}{8}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}},

\displaystyle R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-(l_{i}+\frac{3}{8})s_{0}}\lesssim T^{\frac{3}{8}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}}.

Combining the above four cases, we get

\left|\Phi_{i}(z^{[i]},s)\right|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}+T^{\frac{3}{8}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}}\rangle+Te^{-(l_{i}+1)s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}+e^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}|z^{[i]}|^{\frac{10}{3}l_{i}+\frac{10}{3}}.

In particular,

\left|\Phi_{i}(z^{[i]},s)\right|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle e^{(2l_{i}+\frac{3}{2})s}z^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{1}{4}}+T^{\frac{3}{8}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}e^{-l_{i}s}\langle|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}}\rangle+e^{-(l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}\mbox{ \ for \ }|z^{[i]}|\leq e^{\frac{l_{i}s}{2l_{i}+2}}.

Integrating
|\Phi_{i}|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}
in
\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}\times(s_{0},\infty)
, we attain the conclusion.
∎

Proposition 6.6
.

Under all assumptions in Lemma
6.1
,
then for
\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi,\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}]
given in (
6.4
), there exist
\varphi_{0}(x)\in C^{\infty}_{\rm c}(\overline{\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}})
in (
6.3
) and a constant
C>0
independent of
T,\delta_{0}
such that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi,\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}](x,t)|\leq CR^{-\frac{1}{4}}\Big[\mathbf{1}_{\cap_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\big\{|z^{[i]}|>e^{\frac{l_{i}s}{2l_{i}+2}}\big\}}+\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}(T-t)^{l_{i}}\langle|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{|z^{[i]}|\leq e^{\frac{l_{i}s}{2l_{i}+2}}}\Big],

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi,\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}](x,0)=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}{\bm{b}}_{i}\cdot{\bm{\tilde{e}}}_{i}\big(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(x-q^{[i]})\big)+\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\sum_{\mathbf{p}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},\|\mathbf{p}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4,p_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}}C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p}}\varphi_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0}(x),

where
\bm{b}_{i}={\bm{b}}_{i}[\psi,\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}]
are constant vectors and

|{\bm{b}}_{i}|\leq C|\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2}}
;
{\bm{\tilde{e}}}_{i}
are given in (
6.2
);

C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p}}=C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p}}[\psi,\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}]
are constants and
|C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p}}|\leq Ce^{-\frac{9\delta^{2}}{22T}}
;

\varphi_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0}(x)\in C^{\infty}_{\rm c}(\overline{\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}})
and
\varphi_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0}(x)=0
in
\overline{\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\backslash B_{5}^{+}(q^{[i]},2\delta)}
.
In particular, for
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{N}^{5}
, there exists a constant
C_{\mathbf{m}}>0
independent of
T,\delta_{0}
such that

|D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi,\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}](x,0)|\leq C_{\mathbf{m}}|\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\|\mathbf{m}\|_{\ell_{1}}}{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\mathbf{1}_{|x-q^{[i]}|\leq 2\delta}.

Proof.

Recalling (
6.8
), (
6.10
), we have

\displaystyle\psi_{i}(x,t)={\bm{d}}_{i}(s)\cdot\bm{\tilde{e}}_{i}(z^{[i]})+\Phi_{i}(z^{[i]},s)\quad\mbox{ \ with \ }z^{[i]}=(T-t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(x-q^{[i]}\right),\quad s=-\ln(T-t),

\displaystyle\psi_{i}(x,0)={\bm{b}}_{i}\cdot\bm{\tilde{e}}_{i}\big(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(x-q^{[i]})\big)

with
\bm{b}_{i}={\bm{b}}_{i}[\psi,\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}]=\bm{d}_{i}(s_{0})
given in (
6.12
).
Lemma
6.2
deduces that
|{\bm{b}}_{i}|\lesssim|\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2}}
and
\big|{\bm{d}}_{i}(s)\cdot\bm{\tilde{e}}_{i}(z^{[i]})\big|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{|z^{[i]}|\leq 2C_{\tilde{e}}}se^{-(\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2})s}
.
Combining (
6.25
) and
T^{\frac{3}{8}}\ll R^{-\frac{1}{4}}
due to
T\ll 1
, we have

(6.26)

|\psi_{i}(x,t)|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\Big[(T-t)^{l_{i}}\langle|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{|z^{[i]}|\leq e^{\frac{l_{i}s}{2l_{i}+2}}}+\mathbf{1}_{|z^{[i]}|>e^{\frac{l_{i}s}{2l_{i}+2}}}\Big],\quad i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}.

By uniqueness, we set
\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}=\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(1)}(x,t)+\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}(x,t)
and decompose the equation (
6.6
) of
\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}
into the following two equations.

(6.27)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\psi^{(1)}_{\mathfrak{o}+1}=\Delta_{x}\psi^{(1)}_{\mathfrak{o}+1}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}\times(0,T),\quad-\partial_{x_{5}}\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(1)}=\mathcal{G}_{2,\mathfrak{o}+1}\mbox{ \ on \ }\partial\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}\times(0,T),\quad\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(1)}(x,0)=0\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5},

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}=\Delta_{x}\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}\times(0,T),\quad-\partial_{x_{5}}\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }\partial\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}\times(0,T),\quad\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}(x,0)=\varphi_{0}(x)\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}.

Set

(6.28)

\hat{\psi}_{i}(x,t):=\psi^{(1)}_{\mathfrak{o}+1}(x,t)+\sum_{j=1,j\not=i}^{\mathfrak{o}}\psi_{j}(x,t)\quad\mbox{ \ for \ }i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}.

By (
6.5
), (
6.27
),
\hat{\psi}_{i}
satisfies

(6.29)

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}\hat{\psi}_{i}=\Delta_{x}\hat{\psi}_{i}+h_{1,i}(x,t)\quad\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}\times(0,T),\quad-\partial_{x_{5}}\hat{\psi}_{i}=h_{2,i}(\tilde{x},t)\quad\mbox{ \ on \ }\partial\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}\times(0,T),\\
\hat{\psi}_{i}(x,0)=\sum_{j=1,j\not=i}^{\mathfrak{o}}{\bm{b}}_{j}\cdot{\bm{\tilde{e}}}_{j}\big(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(x-q^{[j]})\big)\quad\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5},\end{cases}

where

h_{1,i}(x,t):=\sum_{j=1,j\not=i}^{\mathfrak{o}}\mathcal{G}_{1,j}(x,t),\quad h_{2,i}(\tilde{x},t):=\sum_{j=1,j\not=i}^{\mathfrak{o}+1}\mathcal{G}_{2,j}(\tilde{x},t).

By the properties of
{\bm{\tilde{e}}}_{j}
given in (
6.2
), (
6.1
),
|\hat{\psi}_{i}(x,0)|\lesssim|\ln T|T^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{j=1,j\not=i}^{\mathfrak{o}}\mathbf{1}_{|x-q^{[j]}|\leq 2C_{\tilde{e}}T^{\frac{1}{2}}}
,
\hat{\psi}_{i}(x,0)\in C_{c}^{\infty}\big(\cup_{j=1,j\not=i}^{\mathfrak{o}}\big\{x\in\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}\ \big|\ |x-q^{[j]}|\leq 2C_{\tilde{e}}T^{\frac{1}{2}}\big\}\big)
.
By Lemma
6.1
, one sees that

\displaystyle\left|h_{1,i}(x,t)\right|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left(T-t\right)^{-4-3l_{\rm{max}}}\mathbf{1}_{\cup_{j=1,j\not=i}^{\mathfrak{o}}\{|x-q^{[j]}|\leq 4\delta\}},

\displaystyle\left|h_{2,i}(\tilde{x},t)\right|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\cap_{j=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\{|\tilde{x}-\tilde{q}^{[j]}|\geq 4\delta\}}+R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left(T-t\right)^{-2-l_{\rm{max}}}\mathbf{1}_{\cup_{j=1,j\not=i}^{\mathfrak{o}}\{|\tilde{x}-\tilde{q}^{[i]}|\leq 8\delta\}},\mbox{ \ with \ }\quad l_{\rm{max}}=\max\limits_{j=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}}l_{j}.

\hat{\psi}_{i}
can be represented by the formula
(
2.17
) with
n=5
and
t_{0}=0
.
Since
h_{1,i}(x,t)=\hat{\psi}_{i}(x,0)=0
for
|x-q^{[i]}|\leq 3\delta
and
h_{2,i}(\tilde{x},t)=0
for
|\tilde{x}-\tilde{q}^{[i]}|\leq 3\delta
, applying Lemma
2.14
with
r=3\delta
, we have

\hat{\psi}_{i}\in C^{\infty}(\overline{B_{5}^{+}(q^{[i]},3\delta/2)}\times[0,T])\mbox{ \ and \ }|\partial_{t}^{\iota}D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\hat{\psi}_{i}|\lesssim_{\iota,\mathbf{m}}e^{-\frac{9\delta^{2}}{22T}}\mbox{ \ in \ }\overline{B_{5}^{+}(q^{[i]},3\delta/2)}\times[0,T]\mbox{ \ for all \ }\iota\in\mathbb{N},\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},

where
\hat{\psi}_{i}
can be defined by extension naturally for
t=T
,
x\in\overline{B_{5}^{+}(q^{[i]},3\delta/2)}
.
Under the additional assumption
m_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}+1
, we have
\partial_{t}^{\iota}D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\hat{\psi}_{i}((\tilde{x},0),t)=0
in
\overline{B_{4}(\tilde{q}^{[i]},3\delta/2)}\times[0,T]
.

By Proposition
2.13
,
for
j=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
,
\mathbf{p}\in\mathbb{N}^{5}
,
\|\mathbf{p}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4
,
p_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}
, we take the vanishing adjustment functions
\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p}}(x,t)
to satisfy

{\partial}_{t}\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p}}=\Delta_{x}\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p}}\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\times(0,T],\quad-{\partial}_{x_{5}}\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p}}=0\mbox{ \ on \ }{\partial}\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\times(0,T],\quad\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p}}(x,0)=\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p},0}(x)\mbox{ \ in \ }\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+},

and the following properties hold:

(1)

\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p},0}(x)

is smooth in
\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}
and
\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p},0}(x)=0
in
\overline{\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\backslash B_{5}^{+}(q^{[j]},2\delta)}
.

(2)

\partial_{t}^{\iota}D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p}}((\tilde{x},0),t)=0
for
\tilde{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{4}
,
t\in[0,T]
, and
\iota\in\mathbb{N}
,
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{N}^{5}
,
m_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}+1
.

(3)

D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p}}(q^{[k]},T)=\delta_{\mathbf{p},\mathbf{m}}\delta_{q^{[j]},q^{[k]}}
for
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{N}^{5}
,
\|\mathbf{m}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4
,
k=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
.

(4)

\|\partial_{t}^{\iota}D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p}}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}\times[0,T])}\lesssim 1
for
\iota\in\mathbb{N}
,
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{N}^{5}
,
2\iota+\|\mathbf{m}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4
.

We take

(6.30)

\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}=\sum_{j=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\sum_{\mathbf{p}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},\|\mathbf{p}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4,p_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}}-\left(D_{x}^{\mathbf{p}}\hat{\psi}_{j}\right)(q^{[j]},T)\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p}}(x,t).

Then for all
i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
,
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{N}^{5}
,
\|\mathbf{m}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4
,
m_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}
, we have
D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\big(\hat{\psi}_{i}+\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}\big)(q^{[i]},T)=0
. One using (
6.27
) and the support of
h_{1,i}
in (
6.29
), it follows that

\partial_{t}^{\iota}D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\big(\hat{\psi}_{i}+\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}\big)(q^{[i]},T)=\Delta_{x}^{\iota}D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\big(\hat{\psi}_{i}+\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}\big)(q^{[i]},T)=0\mbox{ \ for \ }\iota\in\mathbb{N},\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},2\iota+\|\mathbf{m}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4,m_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}.

Besides, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{\iota}D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\big(\hat{\psi}_{i}+\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}\big)(q^{[i]},T)=0\mbox{ \ for \ }\iota\in\mathbb{N},\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},m_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}+1;

\displaystyle\big|\partial_{t}^{\iota}D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\big(\hat{\psi}_{i}+\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}\big)\big|\lesssim e^{-\frac{9\delta^{2}}{22T}}\mbox{ \ for \ }\iota\in\mathbb{N},\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},2\iota+\|\mathbf{m}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4\mbox{ \ in \ }\overline{B_{5}^{+}(q^{[i]},3\delta/2)}\times[0,T].

Applying Taylor expansion to
\hat{\psi}_{i}+\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}
at the point
(q^{[i]},T)
, for
(x,t)\in\overline{B_{5}^{+}(q^{[i]},3\delta/2)}\times[0,T]
, we have

(6.31)

\big|\big(\hat{\psi}_{i}+\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}\big)(x,t)\big|\lesssim e^{-\frac{9\delta^{2}}{22T}}\left[(T-t)^{2l_{\rm{max}}+2}+|x-q^{[i]}|^{2l_{\rm{max}}+2}\right]\lesssim e^{-\frac{9\delta^{2}}{22T}}(T-t)^{l_{i}+1}\langle|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}\rangle.

By (
6.15
),
\left|\mathcal{G}_{2,\mathfrak{o}+1}\right|\leq 1
. Recall the equation of
\psi^{(1)}_{\mathfrak{o}+1}
in (
6.27
).
By the Green’s formula (
2.17
), we have

|\psi^{(1)}_{\mathfrak{o}+1}|\lesssim t^{\frac{1}{2}}
.
By (
6.30
),
|\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}|\lesssim e^{-\frac{9\delta^{2}}{22T}}
. By (
6.26
),
|\psi_{k}|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}
,
k=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
. Hence,

\big|\hat{\psi}_{i}+\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}\big|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mbox{ \ in \ }\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}\times(0,T).

Combining (
6.31
), we get

(6.32)

\big|\hat{\psi}_{i}+\psi_{\mathfrak{o}+1}^{(2)}\big|\lesssim e^{-\frac{9\delta^{2}}{22T}}(T-t)^{l_{i}+1}\langle|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{|x-q^{[i]}|\leq 3\delta/2}+R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mathbf{1}_{|x-q^{[i]}|>3\delta/2}.

Integrating (
6.26
), (
6.32
), and arbitrary choice of
i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
, we complete the proof.
∎

Lemma 6.7
.

Under all assumptions in Lemma
6.1
,
then for
\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi,\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}]
given in (
6.4
),
there exists a constant
C>0
independent of
T,\delta_{0}
such that

(6.33)

|\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi,\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}](x,t)|\leq C\max\{R^{-\frac{1}{4}},\delta_{0}^{\frac{5}{3}}\}\langle x\rangle^{-\frac{7}{3}}\quad\mbox{ \ for \ }|x|\geq 99\max_{i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}}|q^{[i]}|.

Proof.

Recall the equation (
6.3
) of
\psi
, and
\mathcal{G}_{1}=\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\mathcal{G}_{1,i}
,
\mathcal{G}_{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}+1}\mathcal{G}_{2,i}
.
Denote
r_{0}=99\max_{i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}}|q^{[i]}|
.
By Lemma
6.1
and Proposition
6.6
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{1},\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi,\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}](x,0)\equiv 0\mbox{ \ for \ }x\in\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}\backslash B_{5}(0,r_{0}),\quad|\mathcal{G}_{2}|=|\mathcal{G}_{2,\mathfrak{o}+1}|\leq\delta_{0}^{\frac{5}{3}}\langle\tilde{x}\rangle^{-\frac{10}{3}}\mbox{ \ for \ }\tilde{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{4}\backslash B_{4}(0,r_{0}),

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi,\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}](x,t)|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\mbox{ \ for \ }x\in\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}\cap\partial B_{5}(0,r_{0}).

By Lemma
2.8
, set
P(x)=\big|(\tilde{x},x_{5}+1+\vartheta_{1}|\tilde{x}|)\big|^{-\frac{7}{3}}
with a constant
\vartheta_{1}>0
sufficiently small. Then
P(x)
satisfies

-{\partial}_{x_{5}}P\sim\langle x\rangle^{-\frac{10}{3}}
,

\Delta P\lesssim-\langle x\rangle^{-\frac{13}{3}}
.
Thus,
C_{1}\max\{R^{-\frac{1}{4}},\delta_{0}^{\frac{5}{3}}\}P(x)
with a large constant
C_{1}>0
is a barrier function of (
6.3
) in
\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}\backslash B_{5}(0,r_{0})
.
∎

7.
Completion of the proof of Theorem
1.1

Recall
T_{\sigma_{0}}=T-\sigma_{0},\sigma_{0}\in(0,T)
.
In order to avoid the difficulties of the singularity of the right-hand sides at
t=T
(see (
6.16
) for example) and
achieve the compactness for the mappings by the regularity theory,
we solve the outer problem in
B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
,
the orthogonal equations in
(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
, and the inner problems in
B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
in order. Then we get a solution
u_{\sigma_{0}}
of (
1.16
) in
B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
. Finally, we take
\sigma_{0}\downarrow 0
and use the compactness argument to conclude Theorem
1.1
.

7.1.
Solving the outer problem in
B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})

Lemma 7.1
.

Suppose that
\delta_{0}=R^{-\frac{1}{5}}
,
\sigma_{0}\in(0,T)
,

|\mu_{i,1}|\leq(9C_{\mu_{i,0}})^{-1}(T-t)^{2l_{i}+2},|\dot{\mu}_{i,1}|\leq(9C_{\mu_{i,0}})^{-1}(T-t)^{2l_{i}+1}
in
(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})

for
i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
,
\bm{\xi},\dot{\bm{\xi}}
satisfy (
5.5
) in
(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
,

\bm{\phi}\in B_{{\rm in},\sigma_{0}}
,
then for
T\ll 1
, there exists

\psi_{\sigma_{0}}=\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}

solving the outer problem (
3.6
) in
B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
with an initial value
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}(x,0)\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}})
satisfying

\psi_{\sigma_{0}}(x,0)=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}{\bm{b}}_{i,\sigma_{0}}\cdot{\bm{\tilde{e}}}_{i}\big(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(x-q^{[i]})\big)+\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\sum_{\mathbf{p}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},\|\mathbf{p}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4,p_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}}C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},\sigma_{0}}\varphi_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0}(x),

where

{\bm{b}}_{i,\sigma_{0}}={\bm{b}}_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0}},\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]
are constant vectors and
|{\bm{b}}_{i,\sigma_{0}}|\leq C|\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2}}
with a constant
C>0
independent of
T,\sigma_{0}
;
{\bm{\tilde{e}}}_{i}
are given in (
6.2
);

C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},\sigma_{0}}=C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},\sigma_{0}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0}},\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]
are constants
and
|C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},\sigma_{0}}|\leq Ce^{-\frac{9\delta^{2}}{22T}}
;

\varphi_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0}(x)\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}})
and
\varphi_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0}(x)=0
in
\overline{\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\backslash B_{5}^{+}(q^{[i]},2\delta)}
.

For any compact set
K\subset\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}\times[0,T)
, there exists
\sigma_{K}\in(0,T)
sufficiently small such that for all
\sigma_{0}\in(0,\sigma_{K})
,
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}
are well-defined and uniformly Hölder continuous in
K
.

Moreover, there exists a universal constant
\varsigma_{0}\in(0,1)
independent of any parameters such that if
\varsigma_{1}\in(0,\varsigma_{0}]
, there exists a constant
C_{1}>0
independent of
T,\sigma_{0}
such that for all

t_{*}\in(0,T_{\sigma_{0}}),\quad x_{*}\in\overline{B_{5}^{+}(q^{[j]},R^{-1}(T-t_{*})^{1/2})},\quad j=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o},\quad\rho\in(0,R^{-1}(T-t_{*})^{1/2}],

we have

(7.1)

\left[\psi_{\sigma_{0}}\right]_{C^{\varsigma_{1},\varsigma_{1}/2}\big(\overline{B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},\rho)}\times\big(\max\{0,t_{*}-\rho^{2}\},t_{*}\big]\big)}\leq C_{1}\rho^{-\varsigma_{1}}\left[\delta_{0}(T-t_{*})^{l_{j}}+\rho^{2}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t_{*})^{-3l_{j}-4}\right].

Remark 7.1.1
.

Recall
\mathcal{H}_{2,i}
given in (
3.5
). The quantitative Hölder estimate of
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}
is required for the application of
Proposition
1.2
with
n=5
in solving the inner problems.

Proof.

Given a function
f
defined in a domain of
\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\times(0,T)
,
(0,T)
or
\ B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)\times(0,T)
,
denote
f_{\star 1}
,
f_{\star 2}
or
f_{\star 3}
as the zero extension of
f
in
\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\times(0,T)
,
(0,T)
or
\ B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)\times(0,T)
respectively. Denote
\xi^{[i]}_{\star\tilde{q}^{[i]}}(t)=\xi^{[i]}(t)\mathbf{1}_{t\in(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})}+\tilde{q}^{[i]}\mathbf{1}_{t\in[T_{\sigma_{0}},T)}
and
\bm{\xi}_{\star\bm{\tilde{q}}}=(\xi^{[1]}_{\star\tilde{q}^{[1]}},\xi^{[2]}_{\star\tilde{q}^{[2]}},\dots,\xi^{[\mathfrak{o}]}_{\star\tilde{q}^{[\mathfrak{o}]}})
. Denote
\bm{A}=\big(\bm{\mu}_{,0}+(\bm{\mu}_{,1})_{\star 2}
,
\dot{\bm{\mu}}_{,0}+(\dot{\bm{\mu}}_{,1})_{\star 2}
,
\bm{\xi}_{\star\bm{\tilde{q}}}
,
\dot{\bm{\xi}}_{*2}\big)
. It follows that
\bm{A}

satisfies (
5.5
) in
(0,T)
.

By Remark
6.0.1
, we make a distinction between
\bm{\mu}(\bm{\xi})
and
\dot{\bm{\mu}}(\dot{\bm{\xi}})
in Proposition
6.6
and Lemma
6.7
.
For
g\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}
,
we denote
\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[g_{\star 1}]=\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[g_{\star 1},\bm{\phi}_{\star 3},\bm{A}]
for brevity in this proof and the following properties hold

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[g_{\star 1}](x,t)|\lesssim\max\{R^{-\frac{1}{4}},\delta_{0}^{\frac{5}{3}}\}\langle x\rangle^{-\frac{7}{3}}\mathbf{1}_{\cap_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\big\{|z^{[i]}|>e^{\frac{l_{i}s}{2l_{i}+2}}\big\}}+R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}(T-t)^{l_{i}}\langle|z^{[i]}|^{2l_{i}+2}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{|z^{[i]}|\leq e^{\frac{l_{i}s}{2l_{i}+2}}},

\displaystyle|D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[g_{\star 1}](x,0)|\lesssim_{\mathbf{m}}|\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\|\mathbf{m}\|_{\ell_{1}}}{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\mathbf{1}_{|x-q^{[i]}|\leq 2\delta}\quad\mbox{ \ for \ }\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[g_{\star 1}](x,0)=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}{\bm{b}}_{i}\cdot{\bm{\tilde{e}}}_{i}\big(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(x-q^{[i]})\big)+\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\sum_{\mathbf{p}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},\|\mathbf{p}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4,p_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}}C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p}}\varphi_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0}(x),

where

{\bm{b}}_{i}={\bm{b}}_{i}[g_{\star 1},\bm{\phi}_{\star 3},\bm{A}]
are constant vectors and
C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p}}=C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p}}[g_{\star 1},\bm{\phi}_{\star 3},\bm{A}]
are constants, which satisfy

|{\bm{b}}_{i}|\lesssim|\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2}}
and
|C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p}}|\lesssim e^{-\frac{9\delta^{2}}{22T}}
. For
\delta_{0}=R^{-\frac{1}{5}}
and
T\ll 1
, then

\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[g_{\star 1}]\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},0}\cap\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}
.

By
\mathcal{G}_{1}=\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\mathcal{G}_{1,i},\mathcal{G}_{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}+1}\mathcal{G}_{2,i}
, Lemma
6.1
,

\mathcal{G}_{1},\mathcal{G}_{2}
are uniformly bounded for
t\in(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
. One combining the uniform boundedness of
|D_{x}^{\mathbf{m}}\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[g_{\star 1}](x,0)|
with
\mathbf{m}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},\|\mathbf{m}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 1
, by the parabolic regularity theory,
\{\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[g_{\star 1}]\ |\ g\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}\}
is uniformly Hölder continuous in
\overline{B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})}\times[0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
.
Hence,
g\mapsto\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[g_{\star 1}]
is a compact operator from
\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}
to
\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}
.The Schauder fixed-point theorem then yields the existence of a fixed point
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}=\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1}]
in
\overline{B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})}\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
and
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}
, which implies that
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}

solves (
3.6
) in
B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
. Set
\bm{b}_{i,\sigma_{0}}:={\bm{b}}_{i}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1},\bm{\phi}_{\star 3},\bm{A}]
and
C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},\sigma_{0}}:=C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1},\bm{\phi}_{\star 3},\bm{A}]
. Then the initial value has the desired form.

The uniform Hölder continuity of
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}
in compact sets of

\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}\times[0,T)
for
\sigma_{0}
sufficiently small is straightforward by the parabolic estimate.

Finally, we will give the quantitative Hölder estimate of
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}
around the blow-up points.
We always assume
T\ll 1
to make
B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})
sufficiently large to make the following estimates well-defined.
By
\mathcal{G}_{1}=\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\mathcal{G}_{1,i},\mathcal{G}_{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}+1}\mathcal{G}_{2,i}
, Lemma
6.1
, and
\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1}]\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},0}
, for
j=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{G}_{1}|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t)^{-3l_{j}-4}

\displaystyle\mbox{ \ in \ }Q_{\mathcal{G}_{1},j}:=\left\{(x,t)\ |\ t\in(0,T),x\in B_{5}^{+}(q^{[j]},(T-t)^{1/2})\right\},

\displaystyle|\mathcal{G}_{2}|\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t)^{-l_{j}-2}

\displaystyle\mbox{ \ in \ }Q_{\mathcal{G}_{2},j}:=\left\{(\tilde{x},t)\ |\ t\in(0,T),\tilde{x}\in B_{4}(\tilde{q}^{[j]},(T-t)^{1/2})\right\},

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1}]|\lesssim\delta_{0}(T-t)^{l_{j}}

\displaystyle\mbox{ \ in \ }Q_{\mathcal{G}_{1},j}.

Recall the definition of
\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1}]
in (
6.4
). Given

t_{*}\in(0,T),\quad x_{*}\in\overline{B_{5}^{+}(q^{[j]},R^{-1}(T-t_{*})^{1/2})},\quad\rho\in(0,R^{-1}(T-t_{*})^{1/2}],

notice that for any
t_{1}\in\big(\max\{0,t_{*}-4\rho^{2}\},t_{*}\big]
, by
R^{-1}\ll 1
, we have
T-t_{1}\in\left[T-t_{*},2(T-t_{*})\right]
. Then for any
w\in B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},2\rho)
, it holds that
|w-q^{[j]}|\leq|w-x_{*}|+|x_{*}-q^{[j]}|\leq 3R^{-1}(T-t_{*})^{1/2}\leq(T-t_{1})^{1/2}
, which implies
B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},2\rho)\times\big(\max\{0,t_{*}-4\rho^{2}\},t_{*}\big]\subset Q_{\mathcal{G}_{1},j}
. Similarly,

B_{4}(\tilde{x}_{*},2\rho)\times\big(\max\{0,t_{*}-4\rho^{2}\},t_{*}\big]\subset Q_{\mathcal{G}_{2},j}
holds. Hence, for any
(w,t_{1})\in B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},2\rho)\times\big(\max\{0,t_{*}-4\rho^{2}\},t_{*}\big]
, we have
|\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1}](w,t_{1})|\lesssim\delta_{0}(T-t_{1})^{l_{j}}\sim\delta_{0}(T-t_{*})^{l_{j}}
, that is,

\|\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1}]\|_{L^{\infty}\big(B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},2\rho)\times\big(\max\{0,t_{*}-4\rho^{2}\},t_{*}\big]\big)}\lesssim\delta_{0}(T-t_{*})^{l_{j}}.

Similarly, we can deduce that

\displaystyle\rho^{2}\|\mathcal{G}_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}\big(B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},2\rho)\times\big(\max\{0,t_{*}-4\rho^{2}\},t_{*}\big]\big)}\lesssim\rho^{2}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t_{*})^{-3l_{j}-4},

\displaystyle\rho\|\mathcal{G}_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}\big(B_{4}(\tilde{x}_{*},2\rho)\times\big(\max\{0,t_{*}-4\rho^{2}\},t_{*}\big]\big)}\lesssim\rho R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t_{*})^{-l_{j}-2}.

Since for any
w\in B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},2\rho)
,
|w-q^{[j]}|\leq 3R^{-1}(T-t_{*})^{1/2}
, then for any
\alpha\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1}](\cdot,0)\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},2\rho))}\lesssim|\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{j}+\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\rho^{\alpha}[\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1}](\cdot,0)]_{C^{\alpha}(B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},2\rho))}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\rho^{\alpha}\Big[{\bm{b}}_{j,\sigma_{0}}\cdot{\bm{\tilde{e}}}_{j}\big(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}(x-q^{[j]})\big)+\sum_{\mathbf{p}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},\|\mathbf{p}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4,p_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}}C_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p},\sigma_{0}}\varphi_{q^{[j]},\mathbf{p},0}(x)\Big]_{C^{\alpha}(B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},2\rho))}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\rho^{\alpha}\big(|\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{j}+\frac{1}{2}}T^{-\frac{1}{2}}\rho^{1-\alpha}+e^{-\frac{9\delta^{2}}{22T}}\rho^{1-\alpha}\big)\sim\rho|\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{j}}\leq R^{-1}|\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{j}+\frac{1}{2}}.

By Lemma
2.15
,
there exist positive universal constants
\varsigma_{0}\in(0,1),K_{1}
independent of any parameters such that if
\varsigma_{1}\in(0,\varsigma_{0}]
, we have

\displaystyle\left[\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1}]\right]_{C^{\varsigma_{1},\varsigma_{1}/2}\big(\overline{B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},\rho)}\times\big(\max\{0,t_{*}-\rho^{2}\},t_{*}\big]\big)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K_{1}\rho^{-\varsigma_{1}}\Big[\delta_{0}(T-t_{*})^{l_{j}}+\rho^{2}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t_{*})^{-3l_{j}-4}+\mathbf{1}_{x_{*5}\leq 4\rho}\ \rho R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t_{*})^{-l_{j}-2}+\mathbf{1}_{\sqrt{t_{*}}\leq 4\rho}\ |\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{j}+\frac{1}{2}}\Big].

Therein, by the inequality
c_{1}+c_{2}\geq 2\sqrt{c_{1}c_{2}}
for
c_{1},c_{2}\geq 0
, it holds that

\delta_{0}(T-t_{*})^{l_{j}}+\rho^{2}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t_{*})^{-3l_{j}-4}\geq 2(\delta_{0}R^{-\frac{1}{4}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\rho(T-t_{*})^{-l_{j}-2}\geq\rho R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t_{*})^{-l_{j}-2}.

Besides,
\sqrt{t_{*}}\leq 4\rho
,

\rho\in(0,R^{-1}(T-t_{*})^{1/2}]
deduce
t_{*}\leq 16R^{-2}T
. Combining
R^{-1}\ll 1
, then
\mathbf{1}_{\sqrt{t_{*}}\leq 4\rho}\ |\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{j}+\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\delta_{0}(T-t_{*})^{l_{j}}
.
Thus, we get

\left[\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1}]\right]_{C^{\varsigma_{1},\varsigma_{1}/2}\big(\overline{B_{5}^{+}(x_{*},\rho)}\times\big(\max\{0,t_{*}-\rho^{2}\},t_{*}\big]\big)}\lesssim\rho^{-\varsigma_{1}}\left[\delta_{0}(T-t_{*})^{l_{j}}+\rho^{2}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t_{*})^{-3l_{j}-4}\right].

Using
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}=\mathcal{T}^{\rm{out}}[\psi_{\sigma_{0},\star 1}]
in
B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})\times[0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
, we finally achieve (
7.1
). ∎

7.2.
Solving the orthogonal equations in
(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})

In order to apply Proposition
1.2
with
n=5
to solve the inner problems (
3.4
) in
B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
, we need to choose suitable
\bm{\mu},\bm{\xi}
to attain the orthogonality conditions (
1.20
). In other words, we need to solve the following formal orthogonal equations.

(7.2)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}\eta\Big(\frac{y}{4R}\Big)\Big(\dot{\mu}_{i}\mu_{i}Z_{n}(y)+\mu_{i}\dot{\xi}^{[i]}\cdot\left(\nabla_{\tilde{y}}U\right)(\tilde{y},y_{n})\Big)Z_{j}(y)dy

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}\frac{n}{n-2}\mu_{i}^{\frac{n}{2}-1}U^{\frac{2}{n-2}}(\tilde{y},0)\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{4R},0\Big)\Big(\psi_{\sigma_{0}}\big((\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]},0),t\big)+\Theta_{l_{i}}\big((\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]}-\tilde{q}^{[i]},0),t\big)\Big)Z_{j}(\tilde{y},0)d\tilde{y}=0

for
j=1,2,\dots,n
,
t\in(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
,
where
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}=\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}
is given by Lemma
7.1
with
\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}
in a suitable space
to meet the assumption in Lemma
7.1
.
Here we used
y
instead of
y^{[i]}
as the integral variable.

Lemma 7.2
.

Suppose that
\delta_{0}=R^{-\frac{1}{5}}
,
\sigma_{0}\in(0,T)
,

\bm{\phi}\in B_{{\rm in},\sigma_{0}}
,
then for
T\ll 1
, there exist
\bm{\mu}_{,1,\sigma_{0}}=\bm{\mu}_{,1,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}],\bm{\xi}_{\sigma_{0}}=\bm{\xi}_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]
solving (
7.2
) with
n=5
in
(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
and satisfying

(7.3)

|\mu_{i,1,\sigma_{0}}|+|\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}-\tilde{q}^{[i]}|\leq\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{3}}(T-t)^{2l_{i}+2},\quad|\dot{\mu}_{i,1,\sigma_{0}}|+|\dot{\xi}_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}|\leq\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{3}}(T-t)^{2l_{i}+1}

for
t\in[0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
,
i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
. In particular, the ansatz (
5.5
) holds in
[0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
.

Moreover, for any compact set
K\subset[0,T)
, there exists
\sigma_{K}\in(0,T)
sufficiently small such that for all
\sigma_{0}\in(0,\sigma_{K})
,
\dot{\mu}_{i,1,\sigma_{0}}

(\dot{\xi}_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]})
are well-defined and uniformly Hölder continuous in
K
.

Proof.

By the radial property of
\eta(x)
and the parity of
U(y)
and
Z_{j}(y)
given in (
1.13
), (
2.1
) respectively,
we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}\eta(\frac{y}{4R})Z_{j}(y)Z_{k}(y)dy=0\mbox{ \ for \ }j,k=1,2,\dots,n,j\neq k;\ \int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}U^{\frac{2}{n-2}}(\tilde{y},0)\eta(\frac{\tilde{y}}{4R},0)Z_{j}(\tilde{y},0)d\tilde{y}=0\mbox{ \ for \ }j=1,2,\dots,n-1.

Hence, (
7.2
) is equivalent to

(7.4)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \dot{\mu}_{i}=\ &-\frac{n}{n-2}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}Z_{n}^{2}(y)\eta\Big(\frac{y}{4R}\Big)dy\Big)^{-1}\mu_{i}^{\frac{n-4}{2}}\times\\
&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}U^{\frac{2}{n-2}}(\tilde{y},0)\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{4R},0\Big)\Big[\psi_{\sigma_{0}}\big((\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]},0),t\big)+\Theta_{l_{i}}\big((\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]}-\tilde{q}^{[i]},0),t\big)\Big]Z_{n}(\tilde{y},0)d\tilde{y},\end{aligned}

(7.5)

\displaystyle\dot{\xi}^{[i]}=\mathcal{S}^{[i]}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]:=\left(\mathcal{S}_{1}^{[i]}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}],\mathcal{S}_{2}^{[i]}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}],\dots,\mathcal{S}_{n-1}^{[i]}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]\right)

for
i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
, where for
j=1,2,\dots,n-1
,

(7.6)

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{j}^{[i]}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]:=

\displaystyle-\frac{n}{n-2}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}Z_{j}^{2}(y)\eta\Big(\frac{y}{4R}\Big)dy\Big)^{-1}\mu_{i}^{\frac{n-4}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}U^{\frac{2}{n-2}}(\tilde{y},0)\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{4R},0\Big)\Big[\psi_{\sigma_{0}}\big((\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]},0),t\big)

\displaystyle+\Theta_{l_{i}}\big((\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]}-\tilde{q}^{[i]},0),t\big)-\Theta_{l_{i}}(0,t)\Big]Z_{j}(\tilde{y},0)d\tilde{y}.

Recalling (
5.1
) and
\Theta_{l_{i}}(0,t)=-(T-t)^{l_{i}}
, we have

(7.7)

\dot{\mu}_{i,0}=-\frac{n}{n-2}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{n}}Z_{n}^{2}(y)\eta\Big(\frac{y}{4R}\Big)dy\Big)^{-1}\Theta_{l_{i}}(0,t)\mu_{i,0}^{\frac{n-4}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}U^{\frac{2}{n-2}}(\tilde{y},0)\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{4R},0\Big)Z_{n}(\tilde{y},0)d\tilde{y}.

(
7.4
) minus (
7.7
) implies

(7.8)

\dot{\mu}_{i,1}=\mathcal{F}_{i}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}](t)-\frac{n}{n-2}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}Z_{n}^{2}(y)\eta\Big(\frac{y}{4R}\Big)dy\Big)^{-1}\Theta_{l_{i}}(0,t)\frac{n-4}{2}\mu_{i,0}^{\frac{n-6}{2}}\mu_{i,1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}U^{\frac{2}{n-2}}(\tilde{y},0)\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{4R},0\Big)Z_{n}(\tilde{y},0)d\tilde{y},

where

(7.9)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{i}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}](t):=\frac{-n}{n-2}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}_{+}}Z_{n}^{2}(y)\eta\Big(\frac{y}{4R}\Big)dy\Big)^{-1}\bigg\{\mu_{i}^{\frac{n-4}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}U^{\frac{2}{n-2}}(\tilde{y},0)\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{4R},0\Big)\psi_{\sigma_{0}}\big((\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]},0),t\big)Z_{n}(\tilde{y},0)d\tilde{y}

\displaystyle+\mu_{i}^{\frac{n-4}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}U^{\frac{2}{n-2}}(\tilde{y},0)\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{4R},0\Big)\big[\Theta_{l_{i}}\big((\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]}-\tilde{q}^{[i]},0),t\big)-\Theta_{l_{i}}(0,t)\big]Z_{n}(\tilde{y},0)d\tilde{y}

\displaystyle+\Theta_{l_{i}}(0,t)\Big(\mu_{i}^{\frac{n-4}{2}}-\mu_{i,0}^{\frac{n-4}{2}}-\frac{n-4}{2}\mu_{i,0}^{\frac{n-6}{2}}\mu_{i,1}\Big)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}U^{\frac{2}{n-2}}(\tilde{y},0)\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{4R},0\Big)Z_{n}(\tilde{y},0)d\tilde{y}\bigg\}.

By (
5.2
), (
5.3
), then (
7.8
) is equivalent to

(7.10)

\dot{\mu}_{i,1}+\beta(t)\mu_{i,1}=\mathcal{F}_{i}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}](t)\quad\mbox{ \ with \ }\quad\beta(t):=\frac{n-4}{6-n}(l_{i}+1)(T-t)^{-1}.

To obtain a solution of (
7.5
), (
7.10
), it suffices to solve the following fixed-point problem about
\dot{\bm{\mu}}_{,1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}
.

(7.11)

\displaystyle(\dot{\bm{\mu}}_{,1},\dot{\bm{\xi}})=\mathcal{T}^{\rm{ort}}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]:=\big((\mathcal{S}_{1}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}],\mathcal{S}_{2}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}],\dots,\mathcal{S}_{\mathfrak{o}}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]),(\mathcal{S}^{[1]}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}],\mathcal{S}^{[2]}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}],\dots,\mathcal{S}^{[\mathfrak{o}]}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}])\big),

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{i}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]:=\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\int_{T_{\sigma_{0}}}^{t}e^{\int_{t}^{s}\beta(a)da}\mathcal{F}_{i}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}](s)ds\Big)=-\beta(t)\int_{T_{\sigma_{0}}}^{t}e^{\int_{t}^{s}\beta(a)da}\mathcal{F}_{i}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}](s)ds+\mathcal{F}_{i}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}](t),

\displaystyle\mu_{i,1}=\mu_{i,1}[\dot{\mu}_{i,1}](t)=\int_{T_{\sigma_{0}}}^{t}\dot{\mu}_{i,1}(a)da,\quad\xi^{[i]}=\xi^{[i]}[\dot{\xi}^{[i]}](t)=\int_{T_{\sigma_{0}}}^{t}\dot{\xi}^{[i]}(a)da+\tilde{q}^{[i]},\quad i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}.

We will solve
\dot{\bm{\mu}}_{,1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}
in suitable space to make the above integrals and
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}=\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}
given by Lemma
7.1
well-defined.
Set the norm

\|f\|_{l_{i},\sigma_{0}}:=\sup_{s\in(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})}(T-s)^{-(2l_{i}+1)}|f(s)|.

We will find a solution
(\dot{\bm{\mu}}_{,1},\dot{\bm{\xi}})
of (
7.11
) in the space

B_{*1,\sigma_{0}}\times B_{*2,\sigma_{0}}
, where

\displaystyle B_{*1,\sigma_{0}}:=\Big\{(f_{1},f_{2},\dots,f_{\mathfrak{o}})\ \big|\ f_{i}\in C\big((0,T_{\sigma_{0}}),\mathbb{R}\big),i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o},\ \max_{i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}}\|f_{i}\|_{l_{i},\sigma_{0}}\leq\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big\},

\displaystyle B_{*2,\sigma_{0}}:=\Big\{(\mathbf{f}^{[1]},\mathbf{f}^{[2]},\dots,\mathbf{f}^{[\mathfrak{o}]})\ \big|\ \mathbf{f}^{[i]}\in C\big((0,T_{\sigma_{0}}),\mathbb{R}^{n-1}\big),i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o},\ \max_{i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}}\|\mathbf{f}^{[i]}\|_{l_{i},\sigma_{0}}\leq\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big\}.

Hereafter, we plug in
n=5
.
Given any
(\dot{\bm{\mu}}_{1},\dot{\bm{\xi}})\in B_{*1,\sigma_{0}}\times B_{*2,\sigma_{0}}
, for
t\in(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
, we have

(7.12)

|\mu_{i,1}|\leq\int_{t}^{T_{\sigma_{0}}}|\dot{\mu}_{i,1}(a)|da\lesssim\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(T-t)^{2l_{i}+2},\quad|\xi^{[i]}-\tilde{q}^{[i]}|\leq\int_{t}^{T_{\sigma_{0}}}|\dot{\xi}^{[i]}(a)|da\lesssim\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(T-t)^{2l_{i}+2}.

In particular, the integrals about
\mu_{i,1},\xi^{[i]}
in (
7.11
) are well-defined.
Due to the small quantity
\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
T\ll 1
,
\bm{\mu},\dot{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\xi},\dot{\bm{\xi}}
satisfy (
5.5
) in
(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
and the assumption in Lemma
7.1
holds, which implies that
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}=\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}
given by Lemma
7.1
is well-defined.
For
|\tilde{y}|\leq 8R
,
t\in(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
,

|(\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]},0)-q^{[i]}|\leq|\mu_{i}\tilde{y}|+|\xi^{[i]}-\tilde{q}^{[i]}|\lesssim R(T-t)^{2l_{i}+2}\ll(T-t)^{\frac{1}{2l_{i}+2}}.

Combining
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}
and
\Theta_{l_{i}}
given in (
1.14
) with
n=5
, we have

(7.13)

\displaystyle|\psi_{\sigma_{0}}\big((\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]},0),t\big)|\leq\delta_{0}(T-t)^{l_{i}}\big\langle(T-t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}|\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]}-\tilde{q}^{[i]}|\big\rangle^{2l_{i}+2}\sim\delta_{0}(T-t)^{l_{i}},

(7.14)

\displaystyle|\Theta_{l_{i}}\big((\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]}-\tilde{q}^{[i]},0),t\big)-\Theta_{l_{i}}(0,t)|\lesssim(T-t)^{l_{i}}\sum_{j=1}^{l_{i}}\left[(T-t)^{-1}|\mu_{i}\tilde{y}+\xi^{[i]}-\tilde{q}^{[i]}|^{2}\right]^{j}\lesssim R^{2}(T-t)^{5l_{i}+3}.

It then follows from (
5.5
), (
7.13
) and (
7.14
) that
\mathcal{S}_{j}^{[i]}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]
given in (
7.6
) has the upper bound

(7.15)

|\mathcal{S}_{j}^{[i]}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]|\lesssim\delta_{0}(T-t)^{2l_{i}+1}.

By (
5.4
), (
7.12
), and
\delta_{0}\ll 1
,

|\mu_{i}^{\frac{1}{2}}-\mu_{i,0}^{\frac{1}{2}}-2^{-1}\mu_{i,0}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mu_{i,1}|\lesssim\mu_{i,0}^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mu_{i,1}^{2}\lesssim\delta_{0}(T-t)^{l_{i}+1}.

Combining (
5.5
), (
7.13
), (
7.14
),
\mathcal{F}_{i}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}](t)
given in (
7.9
) has the upper bound

(7.16)

|\mathcal{F}_{i}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}](t)|\lesssim\delta_{0}(T-t)^{2l_{i}+1}.

This together with
\beta(t)
given in (
7.10
) implies that for
t\in(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
,

(7.17)

\Big|\beta(t)\int_{T_{\sigma_{0}}}^{t}e^{\int_{t}^{s}\beta(a)da}\mathcal{F}_{i}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}](s)ds\Big|\lesssim(T-t)^{-1}\int_{t}^{T_{\sigma_{0}}}e^{(l_{i}+1)\int_{t}^{s}(T-a)^{-1}da}\delta_{0}(T-s)^{2l_{i}+1}ds\lesssim\delta_{0}(T-t)^{2l_{i}+1}.

By (
7.15
), (
7.16
), (
7.17
), for
\delta_{0}\ll 1
, we deduce that

\mathcal{T}^{\rm{ort}}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]\in B_{*1,\sigma_{0}}\times B_{*2,\sigma_{0}}
.

For all
(\dot{\bm{\mu}}_{,1},\dot{\bm{\xi}})\in B_{*1,\sigma_{0}}\times B_{*2,\sigma_{0}}
,
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]
are uniformly Hölder continuous
in
\overline{B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1}/2)}\times[0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
. By (
7.11
),
\mathcal{T}^{\rm{ort}}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]

is uniformly Hölder continuous
in
[0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
. Hence,
(\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi})\mapsto\mathcal{T}^{\rm{ort}}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]
is a compact mapping from
B_{*1,\sigma_{0}}\times B_{*2,\sigma_{0}}
to itself by the Arzelà-Ascoli theorem. By the Schauder fixed-point theorem, we find a solution
(\dot{\bm{\mu}}_{,1,\sigma_{0}},\dot{\bm{\xi}}_{\sigma_{0}})
of (
7.11
) in
B_{*1,\sigma_{0}}\times B_{*2,\sigma_{0}}
. Combining (
7.12
),
\delta_{0}=R^{-\frac{1}{5}}
,
T\ll 1
, and
uniform Hölder continuity of
\mathcal{T}^{\rm{ort}}[\bm{\mu}_{,1},\bm{\xi}]

in
[0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
, we conclude the final estimate.
∎

7.3.
Solving the inner problems in
B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})

Now the inner problems (
3.4
) for
t\in(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
with
n=5
can be rewritten as the following form formally.
For
i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
,

(7.18)

\begin{cases}(\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}])^{2}{\partial}_{t}\phi_{i}=\Delta_{y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}}\phi_{i}+\mathcal{H}_{1,i}[\bm{\phi}](y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]},t)&\mbox{ \ for \ }t\in\left(0,T_{\sigma_{0}}\right),y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}\in B_{5}^{+}(0,2R),\\
-{\partial}_{y_{5,\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}}\phi_{i}=\frac{5}{3}U^{\frac{2}{3}}(y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]})\phi_{i}+\mathcal{H}_{2,i}[\bm{\phi}](\tilde{y}_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]},t)&\mbox{ \ for \ }t\in\left(0,T_{\sigma_{0}}\right),y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}\in B_{4}(0,2R)\times\{0\},\end{cases}

where
\bm{\mu}_{,1,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]
,
\bm{\xi}_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]
,
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]=\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi},\bm{\mu}_{,1,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}],\bm{\xi}_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]]
are given by Lemma
7.2
and Lemma
7.1
respectively,
\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]=\mu_{i,0}+\mu_{i,1,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]
,

y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}=\frac{x-(\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}[\bm{\phi}],0)}{\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]}
,

\tilde{y}_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}
is the first four components of
y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}
,
y_{5,\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}
is the fifth component of
y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}
,

(7.19)

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1,i}[\bm{\phi}](y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]},t)=

\displaystyle\eta\Big(\frac{y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}}{4R}\Big)\Big(\dot{\mu}_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]Z_{5}(y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]})+\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\dot{\xi}_{i,\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}[\bm{\phi}]\cdot\left(\nabla_{\tilde{y}_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}}U\right)(\tilde{y}_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]},y_{5,\sigma_{0}}^{[i]})\Big),

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{2,i}[\bm{\phi}](\tilde{y}_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]},t)=

\displaystyle\frac{5}{3}\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}^{\frac{3}{2}}[\bm{\phi}]U^{\frac{2}{3}}(\tilde{y}_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]},0)\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}}{4R},0\Big)\Big(\Theta_{l_{i}}\big((\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\tilde{y}_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}+\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}[\bm{\phi}]-\tilde{q}^{[i]},0),t\big)

\displaystyle+\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\big((\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\tilde{y}_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}+\xi^{[i]}[\bm{\phi}],0),t\big)\Big).

Lemma 7.3
.

Suppose that
\delta_{0}=R^{-\frac{1}{5}}
,
\sigma_{0}\in(0,T)
,
then for
T\ll 1
, there exists a solution
\bm{\phi}_{\sigma_{0}}=(\phi_{1,\sigma_{0}},\phi_{2,\sigma_{0}},\dots,\phi_{\mathfrak{o},\sigma_{0}})\in B_{{\rm in},\sigma_{0}}
of (
7.18
). Moreover, for
i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
, the initial value
\phi_{i,\sigma_{0}}(\cdot,0)=C_{{\rm in},i,\sigma_{0}}\tilde{Z}_{0}
in
B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)
, where
\tilde{Z}_{0}\in C^{\infty}(\overline{B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)})
and a constant
C_{{\rm in},i,\sigma_{0}}
satisfies
|C_{{\rm in},i,\sigma_{0}}|\leq CT^{4l_{i}+3}R^{\frac{3}{2}}
with a constant
C>0
independent of
T,\sigma_{0}
.

Furthermore, for any compact set
K\subset[0,T)
, there exists
\sigma_{K}\in(0,T)
sufficiently small such that for all
\sigma_{0}\in(0,\sigma_{K})
,
i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
,
\phi_{i,\sigma_{0}}
are well-defined and uniformly
C^{1+\varsigma_{1},(1+\varsigma_{1})/2}
bounded in
\overline{B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)}\times K
with a constant
\varsigma_{1}\in(0,1/10)
independent of
T,\sigma_{0}
.

Proof.

For any
\bm{\phi}\in B_{{\rm in},\sigma_{0}}
,
\bm{\mu}_{,1,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}],\bm{\xi}_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}],\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]
are well-defined.
For
i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
, set the new time variable

(7.20)

\tau_{i,\sigma_{0}}=\tau_{i,\sigma_{0}}(t):=\int_{0}^{t}\left(\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}](s)\right)^{-2}ds+C_{\mu_{i,0}}^{2}T^{-4l_{i}-3}\sim(T-t)^{-4l_{i}-3}\mbox{ \ for \ }t\in(0,T_{\sigma_{0}}),

where we used (
5.5
) for the last step. For brevity, we use
y^{[i]},\tau_{i}
to denote
y_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]},\tau_{i,\sigma_{0}}
in this proof. We set the corresponding inverse function about
\tau_{i}
as

t=t_{i}(\tau_{i})\mbox{ \ for \ }\tau_{i}\in\left(\tau_{i}(0),\tau_{i}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\right),

which is monotonically increasing in
\tau_{i}
.
Since
(\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}])^{2}{\partial}_{t}\phi_{i}=\partial_{\tau_{i}}\phi_{i}
, we consider

(7.21)

\begin{cases}\partial_{\tau_{i}}\phi_{i}=\Delta_{y^{[i]}}\phi_{i}+\mathcal{H}_{1,i}[\bm{\phi}](y^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i}))&\mbox{ \ for \ }\tau_{i}\in\left(\tau_{i}(0),\tau_{i}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\right),y^{[i]}\in\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5},\\
-{\partial}_{y_{5}^{[i]}}\phi_{i}=\frac{5}{3}U^{\frac{2}{3}}(y^{[i]})\phi_{i}+\mathcal{H}_{2,i}[\bm{\phi}](\tilde{y}^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i}))&\mbox{ \ for \ }\tau_{i}\in\left(\tau_{i}(0),\tau_{i}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\right),y^{[i]}\in\partial\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}.\end{cases}

By (
7.20
),
(
5.4
), (
7.3
), we make the following preparation for estimates about
\mathcal{H}_{1,i}[\bm{\phi}],\mathcal{H}_{2,i}[\bm{\phi}]
.

(7.22)

\displaystyle T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\sim\tau_{i}^{-\frac{1}{4l_{i}+3}};\quad T-t_{i}(a_{1})\sim T-t_{i}(a_{2})\quad\mbox{ \ for \ }a_{1},a_{2}\in\left(\tau_{i}(0),\tau_{i}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\right),\ a_{1}\sim a_{2};

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau_{i}}t_{i}(\tau_{i})=\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}^{2}[\bm{\phi}](t_{i}(\tau_{i}));\quad\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}](t_{i}(\tau_{i}))\sim\left(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\right)^{2l_{i}+2}\sim\tau_{i}^{-\frac{2l_{i}+2}{4l_{i}+3}};

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{d}{d\tau_{i}}\Big(\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}](t_{i}(\tau_{i}))\Big)\Big|=\Big|\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\Big)(t_{i}(\tau_{i}))\frac{d}{d\tau_{i}}t_{i}(\tau_{i})\Big|\lesssim(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{6l_{i}+5}\sim\tau_{i}^{-\frac{6l_{i}+5}{4l_{i}+3}};

\displaystyle|\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}[\bm{\phi}](t_{i}(\tau_{i}))-\tilde{q}^{[i]}|\leq\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{3}}(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{2l_{i}+2}\sim\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau_{i}^{-\frac{2l_{i}+2}{4l_{i}+3}};

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{d}{d\tau_{i}}\Big(\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}[\bm{\phi}](t_{i}(\tau_{i}))\Big)\Big|=\Big|\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}[\bm{\phi}]\Big)(t_{i}(\tau_{i}))\frac{d}{d\tau_{i}}t_{i}(\tau_{i})\Big|\lesssim\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{3}}(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{6l_{i}+5}\sim\delta_{0}^{\frac{1}{3}}\tau_{i}^{-\frac{6l_{i}+5}{4l_{i}+3}}.

For
\mathcal{H}_{1,i}[\bm{\phi}]
,

(7.23)

\big|\mathcal{H}_{1,i}[\bm{\phi}](y^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i}))\big|\lesssim(T-t(\tau_{i}))^{4l_{i}+3}\langle y^{[i]}\rangle^{-3}\eta\Big(\frac{y^{[i]}}{4R}\Big)\lesssim R^{\frac{3}{2}}\tau_{i}^{-1}\langle y^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{9}{2}}\eta\Big(\frac{y^{[i]}}{4R}\Big),

where we used the trick in
[
2
, p.19]
for the last step.

For the application of Proposition
1.2
, pointwise and Hölder estimates are required for
\mathcal{H}_{2,i}[\bm{\phi}](\tilde{y}^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i}))
.
For
|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|\leq\tau_{i}^{1/2}
, denote
Q_{\tilde{y}^{[i]},\tau_{i}}:=\left\{(\tilde{v},s)\in\mathbb{R}^{4}\times\left(\tau_{i}(0),\tau_{i}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\right)\ \big|\ |\tilde{v}-\tilde{y}^{[i]}|\leq\frac{|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|}{2},\tau_{i}-\frac{|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|^{2}}{4}\leq s\leq\tau_{i}\right\}
.
For any
(\tilde{v}^{[j]},s_{j})\in Q_{\tilde{y}^{[i]},\tau_{i}}
,
j=1,2
with
s_{2}\leq s_{1}
, it holds that
t_{i}(s_{2})\leq t_{i}(s_{1})
,

|\tilde{v}^{[j]}|\in\big[|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|/2,3|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|/2\big]
,
and

s_{j}\in[\tau_{i}-\frac{|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|^{2}}{4},\tau_{i}]\subset[3\tau_{i}/4,\tau_{i}]
.
Due to
\eta(\frac{\tilde{y}^{[i]}}{4R},0)
,
[\mathcal{H}_{2,i}\big[\bm{\phi}](\cdot,t_{i}(\cdot))\big]_{C^{\alpha,\alpha/2}(Q_{\tilde{y}^{[i]},\tau_{i}})}=0
for
|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|>16R
and
\mathcal{H}_{2,i}[\bm{\phi}](\tilde{y}^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i}))=0
for
|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|>8R
. Hence, we always assume
|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|\leq 16R

in this proof.
By (
7.22
),

(7.24)

\displaystyle\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}^{\frac{3}{2}}[\bm{\phi}](t_{i}(s_{j}))\sim\tau_{i}^{-\frac{3l_{i}+3}{4l_{i}+3}},

\displaystyle\big|\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}^{\frac{3}{2}}[\bm{\phi}](t_{i}(s_{1}))-\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}^{\frac{3}{2}}[\bm{\phi}](t_{i}(s_{2}))\big|=\Big|\frac{d}{ds}\big(\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}^{\frac{3}{2}}[\bm{\phi}](t_{i}(s))\big)\big|_{s=\theta s_{1}+(1-\theta)s_{2}}(s_{1}-s_{2})\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left[\theta s_{1}+(1-\theta)s_{2}\right]^{-\frac{l_{i}+1}{4l_{i}+3}}\left[\theta s_{1}+(1-\theta)s_{2}\right]^{-\frac{6l_{i}+5}{4l_{i}+3}}|s_{1}-s_{2}|\sim\tau_{i}^{-\frac{7l_{i}+6}{4l_{i}+3}}|s_{1}-s_{2}|

with some
\theta\in[0,1]
. In this proof,
\theta\in[0,1]
will be used repetitively and will vary from line to line.

Recall
U(x)
given in (
1.13
) with
n=5
.

(7.25)

\displaystyle U^{\frac{2}{3}}(\tilde{v}^{[j]},0)\sim\langle\tilde{v}^{[j]}\rangle^{-2}\sim\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-2},

\displaystyle\big|U^{\frac{2}{3}}(\tilde{v}^{[1]},0)-U^{\frac{2}{3}}(\tilde{v}^{[2]},0)\big|=3\left[\theta|\tilde{v}^{[1]}|^{2}+(1-\theta)|\tilde{v}^{[2]}|^{2}+1\right]^{-2}\left||\tilde{v}^{[1]}|^{2}-|\tilde{v}^{[2]}|^{2}\right|\lesssim\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-3}|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|.

\displaystyle\Big|\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{v}^{[1]}}{4R},0\Big)-\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{v}^{[2]}}{4R},0\Big)\Big|\lesssim R^{-1}|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|\mathbf{1}_{|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|\leq 16R}.

Denote
w^{[j]}=\big(\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}](t_{i}(s_{j}))\tilde{v}^{[j]}+\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}[\bm{\phi}](t_{i}(s_{j}))-\tilde{q}^{[i]},0\big)
. By (
7.22
), for
|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|\leq 16R
,

(7.26)

\displaystyle|w^{[j]}|\lesssim\left(T-t_{i}(s_{j})\right)^{2l_{i}+2}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle\sim\left(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\right)^{2l_{i}+2}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle\ll R^{-1}(T-t_{i}(s_{j}))^{1/2},

\displaystyle|w^{[1]}-w^{[2]}|\lesssim(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{6l_{i}+5}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle|s_{1}-s_{2}|+\left(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\right)^{2l_{i}+2}|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|,

\displaystyle|t_{i}(s_{1})-t_{i}(s_{2})|\lesssim(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{4l_{i}+4}|s_{1}-s_{2}|,

where in the second line,
(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{6l_{i}+5}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle|s_{1}-s_{2}|\ll T\left(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\right)^{2l_{i}+2}
.
Recall
\Theta_{l_{i}}
given in (
1.14
) with
n=5
.
By (
7.26
), (
7.22
),

(7.27)

\displaystyle\big|\Theta_{l_{i}}\big(w^{[j]},t_{i}(s_{j})\big)\big|\sim(T-t_{i}(s_{j}))^{l_{i}}\sim\tau_{i}^{-\frac{l_{i}}{4l_{i}+3}},

\displaystyle\big|\Theta_{l_{i}}\big(w^{[1]},t_{i}(s_{1})\big)-\Theta_{l_{i}}\big(w^{[2]},t_{i}(s_{2})\big)\big|=\left(L_{l_{i}}^{\frac{3}{2}}(0)\right)^{-1}\bigg|\left[(T-t_{i}(s_{1}))^{l_{i}}-(T-t_{i}(s_{2}))^{l_{i}}\right]L_{l_{i}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\frac{|w^{[1]}|^{2}}{4(T-t_{i}(s_{1}))}\right)

\displaystyle+(T-t_{i}(s_{2}))^{l_{i}}\bigg[L_{l_{i}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\frac{|w^{[1]}|^{2}}{4(T-t_{i}(s_{1}))}\right)-L_{l_{i}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\frac{|w^{[2]}|^{2}}{4(T-t_{i}(s_{2}))}\right)\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{d}{ds}(T-t_{i}(s))^{l_{i}}\big|_{s=\theta s_{1}+(1-\theta)s_{2}}(s_{1}-s_{2})\Big|+(T-t_{i}(s_{2}))^{l_{i}}\bigg|\frac{|w^{[1]}|^{2}}{T-t_{i}(s_{1})}-\frac{|w^{[2]}|^{2}}{T-t_{i}(s_{2})}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(T-t_{i}(\theta s_{1}+(1-\theta)s_{2})\big)^{5l_{i}+3}|s_{1}-s_{2}|

\displaystyle+(T-t_{i}(s_{2}))^{l_{i}}\bigg|\frac{|w^{[1]}|^{2}(t_{i}(s_{1})-t_{i}(s_{2}))}{(T-t_{i}(s_{1}))(T-t_{i}(s_{2}))}+\frac{(|w^{[1]}|+|w^{[2]}|)(|w^{[1]}|-|w^{[2]}|)}{T-t_{i}(s_{2})}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\big)^{5l_{i}+3}|s_{1}-s_{2}|+(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{l_{i}}\Big\{\left(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\right)^{8l_{i}+6}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{2}|s_{1}-s_{2}|

\displaystyle+\left(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\right)^{2l_{i}+1}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle\left[(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{6l_{i}+5}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle|s_{1}-s_{2}|+\left(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\right)^{2l_{i}+2}|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|\right]\Big\}

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\big(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\big)^{5l_{i}+3}\left(|s_{1}-s_{2}|+\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|\right)\sim\tau_{i}^{-\frac{5l_{i}+3}{4l_{i}+3}}\left(|s_{1}-s_{2}|+\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|\right).

By (
7.26
),
T\ll 1
makes
w^{[j]}+q^{[i]}\in\overline{B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})}
.
Using

\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},\sigma_{0}}

and
|w^{[j]}|\ll R^{-1}(T-t_{i}(s_{j}))^{1/2}
in (
7.26
),
we have

(7.28)

\big|\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\big(w^{[j]}+q^{[i]},t_{i}(s_{j})\big)\big|\lesssim\delta_{0}(T-t_{i}(s_{j}))^{l_{i}}\sim\delta_{0}\tau_{i}^{-\frac{l_{i}}{4l_{i}+3}}.

If
\max\{|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|,|s_{1}-s_{2}|\}>1
,
by (
7.28
),
\delta_{0}=R^{-\frac{1}{5}}
,
|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|\leq 16R
, for any
\varsigma_{1}\in(0,1/10)
, we have

(7.29)

\frac{\big|\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\big(w^{[1]}+q^{[i]},t_{i}(s_{1})\big)-\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\big(w^{[2]}+q^{[i]},t_{i}(s_{2})\big)\big|}{\left(\max\left\{|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|,|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{1/2}\right\}\right)^{\varsigma_{1}}}\lesssim\delta_{0}\tau_{i}^{-\frac{l_{i}}{4l_{i}+3}}\lesssim R^{-\frac{1}{10}}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-\varsigma_{1}}\tau_{i}^{-\frac{l_{i}}{4l_{i}+3}}.

If
\max\{|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|,|s_{1}-s_{2}|\}\leq 1
, by (
7.26
) and
T-t_{i}(s_{j})\sim T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})
, we have

|w^{[1]}|\leq R^{-1}(T-t_{i}(s_{1}))^{1/2}
,

|w^{[1]}-w^{[2]}|+|t_{i}(s_{1})-t_{i}(s_{2})|^{1/2}\lesssim\left(T-t_{i}(s_{1})\right)^{2l_{i}+2}
.
Hence,
by the Hölder estimate (
7.1
) with
(x_{*},t_{*})=(w^{[1]}+q^{[i]},t_{i}(s_{1}))
,
\rho=\left(T-t_{i}(s_{1})\right)^{2l_{i}+2}\ln R
, where obviously
\left(T-t_{i}(s_{1})\right)^{2l_{i}+2}\ll\rho\leq R^{-1}(T-t_{i}(s_{1}))^{1/2}
, then there exists a constant
\varsigma_{1}\in(0,1/10)
independent of
T,\sigma_{0}

such that

(7.30)

\displaystyle\big|\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\big(w^{[1]}+q^{[i]},t_{i}(s_{1})\big)-\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]\big(w^{[2]}+q^{[i]},t_{i}(s_{2})\big)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\rho^{-\varsigma_{1}}\left[\delta_{0}(T-t_{i}(s_{1}))^{l_{i}}+\rho^{2}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t_{i}(s_{1}))^{-3l_{i}-4}\right]\left(\max\left\{|w^{[1]}-w^{[2]}|,|t_{i}(s_{1})-t_{i}(s_{2})|^{1/2}\right\}\right)^{\varsigma_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\rho^{-\varsigma_{1}}\left[\delta_{0}(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{l_{i}}+\rho^{2}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{-3l_{i}-4}\right]\left(\left(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\right)^{2l_{i}+2}\max\left\{|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|,|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{1/2}\right\}\right)^{\varsigma_{1}}

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle(\ln R)^{-\varsigma_{1}}\left(\delta_{0}+(\ln R)^{2}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right)\left(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\right)^{l_{i}}\left(\max\left\{|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|,|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{1/2}\right\}\right)^{\varsigma_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle R^{-\frac{1}{10}}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-\varsigma_{1}}\tau_{i}^{-\frac{l_{i}}{4l_{i}+3}}\left(\max\left\{|\tilde{v}^{[1]}-\tilde{v}^{[2]}|,|s_{1}-s_{2}|^{1/2}\right\}\right)^{\varsigma_{1}},

where we used (
7.26
) for the second “
\lesssim
”.

Combining (
7.24
), (
7.25
), (
7.27
),
(
7.28
), (
7.29
), (
7.30
),
we obtain

(7.31)

\displaystyle\big|\mathcal{H}_{2,i}[\bm{\phi}](\tilde{y}^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i}))\big|\lesssim\tau_{i}^{-1}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-2}\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}^{[i]}}{4R},0\Big)\lesssim R^{\frac{3}{2}}\tau_{i}^{-1}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{7}{2}}\eta\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}^{[i]}}{4R},0\Big),

\displaystyle[\mathcal{H}_{2,i}\big[\bm{\phi}](\cdot,t_{i}(\cdot))\big]_{C^{\varsigma_{1},\varsigma_{1}/2}(Q_{\tilde{y}^{[i]},\tau_{i}})}\lesssim\tau_{i}^{-1}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|\leq 16R}\Big[\tau_{i}^{-1}|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|^{2-\varsigma_{1}}+\left(\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-1}+\tau_{i}^{-1}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle\right)|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|^{1-\varsigma_{1}}

\displaystyle+R^{-\frac{1}{10}}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-\varsigma_{1}}\Big]\lesssim\tau_{i}^{-1}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-2-\varsigma_{1}}\mathbf{1}_{|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|\leq 16R}\lesssim R^{\frac{3}{2}}\tau_{i}^{-1}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{7}{2}-\varsigma_{1}}\mathbf{1}_{|\tilde{y}^{[i]}|\leq 16R}.

Recalling the norms given in (
1.17
),
by (
7.23
), (
7.31
), we have

\|\mathcal{H}_{1,i}[\bm{\phi}](\cdot,t_{i}(\cdot))\|_{-1,\frac{9}{2},\tau_{i}^{p},\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+},\tau_{i}(0),\tau_{i}(T_{\sigma_{0}})}\lesssim R^{\frac{3}{2}}
,

\|\mathcal{H}_{2,i}[\bm{\phi}](\cdot,t_{i}(\cdot))\|_{-1,\frac{7}{2},\tau_{i}^{p},\varsigma_{1},\mathbb{R}^{4},\tau_{i}(0),\tau_{i}(T_{\sigma_{0}})}\lesssim R^{\frac{3}{2}}
provided
\tau_{i}^{p}>16R
.
The choice of
\bm{\mu}_{,1,\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}],\bm{\xi}_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]
in Lemma
7.2
meets the orthogonal equations (
7.2
). Namely,
\mathcal{H}_{1,i}[\bm{\phi}](y^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i})),\mathcal{H}_{2,i}[\bm{\phi}](\tilde{y}^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i}))
satisfy the orthogonality conditions (
1.20
) with
n=5
for
\tau_{i}\in\left(\tau_{i}(0),\tau_{i}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\right)
.
Thus, for
T\ll 1
, Proposition
1.2
with
n=5,\tau=\tau_{i}\in\big(\tau_{i}(0),\tau_{i}(T_{\sigma_{0}})\big)
,

\sigma=-1,a=\frac{5}{2},\ell(\tau)=\tau_{i}^{p},p\in(\frac{2}{5},\frac{3}{5}),\iota\in\big(\frac{1}{10}(\frac{5}{2}p+1),\frac{1}{4}\big)
,
\varsigma=\varsigma_{1}
,
g=\mathcal{H}_{1,i}[\bm{\phi}](y^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i}))
,

h=\mathcal{H}_{2,i}[\bm{\phi}](\tilde{y}^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i}))
,
and
\tilde{Z}_{0}\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}})
satisfying (
1.21
) (see Remark
1.2.1
for the existence of
\tilde{Z}_{0}
)
yields a mapping
\mathcal{T}_{i}^{{\rm in}}[\bm{\phi}]=\mathcal{T}_{i}^{{\rm in}}[\bm{\phi}](y^{[i]},\tau_{i})
with the estimate

|\mathcal{T}_{i}^{{\rm in}}[\bm{\phi}]|+\langle y^{[i]}\rangle|\nabla_{y^{[i]}}\mathcal{T}_{i}^{{\rm in}}[\bm{\phi}]|\lesssim R^{\frac{3}{2}}\tau_{i}^{-1}\langle y^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{5}{2}}\sim R^{-\frac{1}{2}}R^{2}(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{4l_{i}+3}\langle y^{[i]}\rangle^{-\frac{5}{2}}

for
(y^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i}))\in B_{5}^{+}(0,9R)\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}})
.
For solving (
7.18
), it suffices to solve the fixed-point problem

\bm{\phi}=\mathcal{T}^{{\rm in}}[\bm{\phi}]:=\big(\mathcal{T}_{1}^{{\rm in}}[\bm{\phi}](y^{[1]},\tau_{1}(t)),\mathcal{T}_{2}^{{\rm in}}[\bm{\phi}](y^{[2]},\tau_{2}(t)),\dots,\mathcal{T}_{\mathfrak{o}}^{{\rm in}}[\bm{\phi}](y^{[\mathfrak{o}]},\tau_{\mathfrak{o}}(t))\big)

for
t\in\left(0,T_{\sigma_{0}}\right),y^{[i]}\in B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)
,
i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}
.
Due to the small quantity
R^{-\frac{1}{2}}
,
\mathcal{T}^{{\rm in}}[\bm{\phi}]\in B_{{\rm in},\sigma_{0}}
.

For all
\bm{\phi}\in B_{{\rm in},\sigma_{0}}
,
the uniform Hölder continuity of
\nabla_{y^{[i]}}\mathcal{T}_{i}^{{\rm in}}[\bm{\phi}]

in
t\in\left(0,T_{\sigma_{0}}\right),y^{[i]}\in\overline{B_{5}^{+}(0,8R)}
,
i=1,2,\dots,\mathfrak{o}

follows from the parabolic regularity theory. Hence
\mathcal{T}^{\rm in}[\cdot]
is a compact mapping from
B_{{\rm in},\sigma_{0}}
to itself. The Schauder fixed-point theorem gives a fixed point
\bm{\phi}_{\sigma_{0}}=(\phi_{1,\sigma_{0}},\phi_{2,\sigma_{0}},\dots,\phi_{\mathfrak{o},\sigma_{0}})\in B_{{\rm in},\sigma_{0}}
. Moreover, Proposition
1.2
gives
\phi_{i,\sigma_{0}}(\cdot,0)=C_{{\rm in},i,\sigma_{0}}\tilde{Z}_{0}
in
B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)
with a constant
C_{{\rm in},i,\sigma_{0}}
satisfying
|C_{{\rm in},i,\sigma_{0}}|\lesssim(\tau_{i}(0))^{-1}R^{\frac{3}{2}}\sim T^{4l_{i}+3}R^{\frac{3}{2}}
.
The last uniform
C^{1+\varsigma_{1},(1+\varsigma_{1})/2}
boundedness in
\overline{B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)}\times K
follows from (
7.23
), (
7.31
), and the parabolic regularity theory.
∎

Remark 7.3.1
.

We did not apply the Hölder estimate (
7.1
) to
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}[\bm{\phi}]
with
(x_{*},t_{*})=(q^{[i]},t_{i}(\tau_{i}))
. Since due to (
7.26
), we require
\rho\gtrsim\left(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i})\right)^{2l_{i}+2}\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle
. Then the term
\rho^{2-\varsigma_{1}}R^{-\frac{1}{4}}(T-t_{i}(\tau_{i}))^{-3l_{i}-4}
will lead to additional spatial growth
\langle\tilde{y}^{[i]}\rangle^{2}
, which can not be eliminated by
R^{-\frac{1}{4}}
.

7.4.
Proof of Theorem
1.1

Proof of Theorem
1.1
.

Combining Lemma
7.1
, Lemma
7.2
, Lemma
7.3
, for
\sigma_{0}\in(0,T)
, we set

(7.32)

v_{\sigma_{0}}(x,t):=\psi_{\sigma_{0}}(x,t)+\sum_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\bigg(U_{\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}},\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}}(x)\eta\Big(\frac{x^{[i]}}{2\delta}\Big)+\Theta_{l_{i}}(x^{[i]},t)\eta\Big(\frac{x^{[i]}}{\delta}\Big)+\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}^{-\frac{3}{2}}\phi_{i,\sigma_{0}}\Big(\frac{x-\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}}{\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}},t\Big)\eta\Big(\frac{x-\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}}{\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}R}\Big)\bigg)

with
\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}=\mu_{i,0}+\mu_{i,1,\sigma_{0}}
, and then
v_{\sigma_{0}}
satisfies

(7.33)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}v_{\sigma_{0}}=\Delta v_{\sigma_{0}}\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}}),\quad\left(-\partial_{x_{5}}v_{\sigma_{0}}\right)(\tilde{x},0)=\big(|v_{\sigma_{0}}|^{\frac{2}{3}}v_{\sigma_{0}}\big)(\tilde{x},0)\mbox{ \ on \ }B_{4}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1})\times(0,T_{\sigma_{0}}),\\
&v_{\sigma_{0}}(x,0)=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{\mathfrak{o}}\bigg[{\bm{b}}_{i,\sigma_{0}}\cdot{\bm{\tilde{e}}}_{i}\big(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}x^{[i]}\big)+\sum_{\mathbf{p}\in\mathbb{N}^{5},\|\mathbf{p}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq 4l_{\rm{max}}+4,p_{5}\in 2\mathbb{N}}C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},\sigma_{0}}\varphi_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0}(x)+U_{\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}(0),\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}(0)}(x)\eta\Big(\frac{x^{[i]}}{2\delta}\Big)\\
&\quad+\Theta_{l_{i}}(x^{[i]},0)\eta\Big(\frac{x^{[i]}}{\delta}\Big)+\big(\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}(0)\big)^{-\frac{3}{2}}C_{{\rm in},i,\sigma_{0}}\tilde{Z}_{0}\Big(\frac{x-\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}(0)}{\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}(0)}\Big)\eta\Big(\frac{x-\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}(0)}{\mu_{i,\sigma_{0}}(0)R}\Big)\bigg]\mbox{ \ in \ }B_{5}^{+}(0,\sigma_{0}^{-1}),\end{aligned}\right.

where

{\bm{b}}_{i,\sigma_{0}}
are constant vectors and
C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},\sigma_{0}}
,
C_{{\rm in},i,\sigma_{0}}
are constants satisfying

|{\bm{b}}_{i,\sigma_{0}}|\leq C|\ln T|T^{\frac{5}{3}l_{i}+\frac{1}{2}}
,
|C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},\sigma_{0}}|\leq Ce^{-\frac{9\delta^{2}}{22T}}
, and
|C_{{\rm in},i,\sigma_{0}}|\leq CT^{4l_{i}+3}R^{\frac{3}{2}}
with a constant
C
independent of
T,\sigma_{0}
;
{\bm{\tilde{e}}}_{i}
are given in (
6.2
);
\varphi_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0}\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}})
and
\varphi_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0}=0
in
\overline{\mathbb{R}^{5}_{+}\backslash B_{5}^{+}(q^{[i]},2\delta)}
;

\tilde{Z}_{0}\in C^{\infty}(\overline{B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)})
.

Up to a subsequence, there exist constant vectors
{\bm{b}}_{i,0}
and constants
C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0},C_{{\rm in},i,0}
such that
{\bm{b}}_{i,\sigma_{0}}\to{\bm{b}}_{i,0}
,
C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},\sigma_{0}}\to C_{q^{[i]},\mathbf{p},0},C_{{\rm in},i,\sigma_{0}}\to C_{{\rm in},i,0}
as
\sigma_{0}\downarrow 0
. For simplicity of exposition, we will often take a subsequence with
\sigma_{0}\downarrow 0
but will not state it.
By Lemma
7.1
, by the diagonal method, there exists a function
\psi_{0}
such that
\psi_{\sigma_{0}}\to\psi_{0}
in
L_{\rm{loc}}^{\infty}(\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}\times[0,T))
, and then
\psi_{0}\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta_{0},0}
. Similarly,
by Lemma
7.2
,
\mu_{i,1,\sigma_{0}}\to\mu_{i,1,0},\xi_{\sigma_{0}}^{[i]}\to\xi_{0}^{[i]}
in
C_{\rm{loc}}^{1}([0,T))
,
and then
\mu_{i,1,0},\xi_{0}^{[i]}
satisfy (
7.3
) with
\sigma_{0}=0
. Denote
\mu_{i,0}=\mu_{i,0}+\mu_{i,1,0}
.
By Lemma
7.3
,

\phi_{i,\sigma_{0}}\to\phi_{i,0}
and
\nabla\phi_{i,\sigma_{0}}\to\nabla\phi_{i,0}

in

L^{\infty}\big(\overline{B_{5}^{+}(0,2R)}\times K\big)
for any compact set
K\subset[0,T)
, and then
\phi_{i,0}\in B_{{\rm in},0}
.

Now we can extend the definition (
7.32
) of
v_{\sigma_{0}}
at
\sigma_{0}=0
naturally. Then
v_{0}(x,0)\in C_{c}^{\infty}(\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}})
. By the convergence argument above,
v_{\sigma_{0}}\to v_{0}
in
L_{\rm{loc}}^{\infty}(\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}\times[0,T))
and

v_{\sigma_{0}}(x,0)\to v_{0}(x,0)
in
C_{\rm{loc}}^{k}(\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}})
for any
k\in\mathbb{N}
. One testing (
7.33
) with arbitrary functions in
C_{c}^{\infty}(\overline{\mathbb{R}_{+}^{5}}\times[0,T))
with
\sigma_{0}
sufficiently small, then taking
\sigma_{0}\downarrow 0
deduces that
v_{0}
is a weak solution of
(
1.16
). Set
u=v_{0},\mu_{i}=\mu_{i,0},\xi^{[i]}=\xi_{0}^{[i]}
, and then we get Theorem
1.1
.
∎

Remark 7.3.2
.

We can not use contraction mapping like
[
5
]
to solve
\dot{\bm{\mu}}_{,1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}
under the current topology since we do not have gradient estimate of
\psi
and can not get the Lipschitz continuity about
\dot{\bm{\mu}}_{,1},\dot{\bm{\xi}}
in (
7.11
) with
T_{\sigma_{0}}=T
.

Appendix A
A type I solution of (
1.2
)

Proposition A.1
.

Suppose
\alpha\in(0,1)
,
p>1
, then

u_{1}
given in (
1.7
) satisfies (
1.2
) with
\left(t_{0},t_{1}\right)=\left(-\infty,T\right)
and (
1.8
).

Proof.

By
[
54
, Corollary 1.4]
, for
t<T
,

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{t}-\Delta\right)^{\alpha}\left(T-t\right)^{a}=\left(\partial_{t}\right)^{\alpha}\left(T-t\right)^{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\left|\Gamma(-\alpha)\right|}\int_{-\infty}^{t}\frac{\left(T-t\right)^{a}-\left(T-\tau\right)^{a}}{\left(t-\tau\right)^{1+\alpha}}d\tau=\frac{1}{\left|\Gamma(-\alpha)\right|}\left(T-t\right)^{a-\alpha}\int_{1}^{\infty}\frac{1-z^{a}}{\left(z-1\right)^{1+\alpha}}dz,

where we changed the variable
z=\frac{T-\tau}{T-t}
, and
\max\left\{0,a\right\}<\alpha<1
is required for the integrability.

Given
u\left(\tilde{x},0,t\right)
of the form
C\left(T-t\right)^{a}
, by
[
54
, Theorem 1.7, (1.7)]
, in order to make

\frac{\left|\Gamma(-\alpha)\right|}{4^{\alpha}\Gamma(\alpha)}\left(\partial_{t}-\Delta_{\tilde{x}}\right)^{\alpha}\left[C\left(T-t\right)^{a}\right]=\left|C\left(T-t\right)^{a}\right|^{p-1}C\left(T-t\right)^{a},

we take
a=\frac{-\alpha}{p-1}
with
p>1
, and
C=\pm C_{\alpha,p}
with
C_{\alpha,p}>0
given in (
1.7
). Thus

u\left(\tilde{x},0,t\right)=\pm C_{\alpha,p}\left(T-t\right)^{\frac{-\alpha}{p-1}}
.
By
[
54
, Theorem 1.7, (1.5)]
, for
x_{n}>0
,

\displaystyle u_{1}\left(\tilde{x},x_{n},t\right)=\frac{x_{n}^{2\alpha}}{4^{\alpha}\Gamma(\alpha)}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\frac{x_{n}^{2}}{4\tau}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n-1}}\left(4\pi\tau\right)^{-\frac{n-1}{2}}e^{-\frac{|z|^{2}}{4\tau}}\left(\pm C_{\alpha,p}\right)\left(T-t+\tau\right)^{\frac{-\alpha}{p-1}}dz\tau^{-1-\alpha}d\tau

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{x_{n}^{2\alpha}}{4^{\alpha}\Gamma(\alpha)}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\frac{x_{n}^{2}}{4\tau}}\left(\pm C_{\alpha,p}\right)\left(T-t+\tau\right)^{\frac{-\alpha}{p-1}}\tau^{-1-\alpha}d\tau=\frac{\pm C_{\alpha,p}}{\Gamma(\alpha)}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-s}\left(T-t+\frac{x_{n}^{2}}{4s}\right)^{\frac{-\alpha}{p-1}}s^{\alpha-1}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\pm C_{\alpha,p}}{\Gamma(\alpha)}\left(\frac{x_{n}^{2}}{4}\right)^{\frac{-\alpha}{p-1}}\bigg(\int_{0}^{\frac{x_{n}^{2}}{T-t}}+\int_{\frac{x_{n}^{2}}{T-t}}^{\infty}\bigg)e^{-s}s^{\alpha-1}\left[\frac{4(T-t)}{x_{n}^{2}}+s^{-1}\right]^{\frac{-\alpha}{p-1}}ds

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\frac{\pm C_{\alpha,p}}{\Gamma(\alpha)}\left(\frac{x_{n}^{2}}{4}\right)^{\frac{-\alpha}{p-1}}\bigg[\int_{0}^{\frac{x_{n}^{2}}{T-t}}e^{-s}s^{\alpha-1+\frac{\alpha}{p-1}}ds+\left(\frac{T-t}{x_{n}^{2}}\right)^{\frac{-\alpha}{p-1}}\int_{\frac{x_{n}^{2}}{T-t}}^{\infty}e^{-s}s^{\alpha-1}ds\bigg]\sim\pm\left(\max\left\{T-t,x_{n}^{2}\right\}\right)^{\frac{-\alpha}{p-1}}.

∎

Appendix B
Convolution estimates

B.1.
Inequalities toolkit

Lemma B.1
.

(1)

Given
a>0
,
b>0
, then for any
r>0
,
r^{a}e^{-br}\leq b\int_{r}^{\infty}x^{a}e^{-bx}dx
.
Given
a<0
,
b>0
, then for any
r>0
,
b\int_{r}^{\infty}x^{a}e^{-bx}dx\leq r^{a}e^{-br}
.
Given
a\in\mathbb{R}
,
b>0
,
r_{0}>0
, then for any
r>r_{0}
,

(B.1)

\int_{r}^{\infty}x^{a}e^{-bx}dx\sim C(a,b,r_{0})r^{a}e^{-br},

where
C(a,b,r_{0})>0
is a constant depending on
a,b,r_{0}
, and “
\sim
” does not depend on any parameters.

(2)

Given
a\in\mathbb{R}
,
r_{0}>0
, then for any
A>B\geq r_{0}
,

(B.2)

\int_{B}^{A}r^{a}\left(1+\ln A-\ln r\right)e^{-r}dr\leq\left(1+\ln\left(B^{-1}A\right)\right)\int_{B}^{A}r^{a}e^{-r}dr\leq C(a,r_{0})B^{a}\left(1+\ln\left(B^{-1}A\right)\right)e^{-B}.

(3)

Given
b>0
,
a\geq 0
, then
r^{a}e^{br}
is monotone increasing in
r>0
. Given
b>0
,
a<0
,
r_{0}>0
, then for any
r_{2}>r_{1}\geq r_{0}
,

(B.3)

r_{1}^{a}e^{br_{1}}\leq C(a,b,r_{0})r_{2}^{a}e^{br_{2}}

with a constant
C(a,b,r_{0})>0
depending on
a,b,r_{0}
.

Given
b<0
,
a\leq 0
, then
r^{a}e^{br}
is monotone decreasing in
r>0
. Given
b<0
,
a>0
,
r_{0}>0
, then for any
r_{2}>r_{1}\geq r_{0}
,

(B.4)

r_{1}^{a}e^{br_{1}}\geq C(a,b,r_{0})r_{2}^{a}e^{br_{2}}

with a constant
C(a,b,r_{0})>0
depending on
a,b,r_{0}
.

(4)

Given
a<0
,
r_{0}>0
, then for any
r_{2}>r_{1}\geq r_{0}
,

(B.5)

r_{1}^{a}\left(1+\left|\ln r_{1}\right|\right)\geq C(a,r_{0})r_{2}^{a}\left(1+\left|\ln r_{2}\right|\right),

(B.6)

r_{1}^{a}\left(1+\ln\left(r_{0}^{-1}r_{1}\right)\right)\geq C(a)r_{2}^{a}\left(1+\ln\left(r_{0}^{-1}r_{2}\right)\right)

with a constant
C(a,r_{0})>0
depending on
a,r_{0}
and a constant
C(a)>0
depending on
a
.

(5)

Given
a<-1
,
b\in\mathbb{R}
,
r_{0}>0
, then for any
r\geq r_{0}
,

(B.7)

\int_{r}^{\infty}x^{a}\left(1+\left|\ln x\right|\right)^{b}dx\leq C(a,b,r_{0})r^{a+1}\left(1+\left|\ln r\right|\right)^{b}

with a constant
C(a,b,r_{0})>0
depending on
a,b,r_{0}
.

Proof.

(
1
). The first two inequalities are straightforward.
Given
a\in\mathbb{R}
,
b=1
,
r_{0}>0
, for
M>1
,

\int_{r}^{Mr}x^{a}e^{-x}dx\sim C(M,a)r^{a}\int_{r}^{Mr}e^{-x}dx=C(M,a)r^{a}e^{-r}\left[1-e^{-(M-1)r}\right].

One taking
M=M(r_{0})
large to make

(M-1)r_{0}\geq 1
, then

\int_{r}^{Mr}x^{a}e^{-x}dx\sim C(M,a)r^{a}e^{-r}
.
For the other part,

\int_{Mr}^{\infty}x^{a}e^{-x}dx=(Mr)^{a}e^{-Mr}+\int_{Mr}^{\infty}ax^{a-1}e^{-x}dx,\mbox{ \ and \ }\left|\int_{Mr}^{\infty}ax^{a-1}e^{-x}dx\right|\leq\frac{|a|}{Mr_{0}}\int_{Mr}^{\infty}x^{a}e^{-x}dx.

One taking
M=M(|a|,r_{0})
large to make
\frac{|a|}{Mr_{0}}<9^{-1}
, then

\int_{Mr}^{\infty}x^{a}e^{-x}dx\sim(Mr)^{a}e^{-Mr}\leq e^{(1-M)r_{0}}M^{a}r^{a}e^{-r}.

Thus, we conclude (
B.1
) for
b=1
, which implies the general case
b>0
by

\int_{r}^{\infty}x^{a}e^{-bx}dx=b^{-a-1}\int_{br}^{\infty}z^{a}e^{-z}dz\sim C(a,b,r_{0})r^{a}e^{-br}.

(
2
) is deduced by (
1
).

(
3
). For
b>0
,
a<0
,
r_{0}>0
, denote

f(r)=r^{a}e^{br}
, then
f^{\prime}(r)=r^{a}e^{br}\left(b+r^{-1}a\right)
and
f^{\prime}(r)>0
for
r\geq 9|a|b^{-1}
.
For
r_{0}\leq r\leq\max\left\{9|a|b^{-1},r_{0}\right\}
, we have
r^{a}e^{br}\sim C(a,b,r_{0})r_{0}^{a}e^{br_{0}}
. Thus, (
B.3
) holds.

The inequality for the case
b<0
,
a>0
,
r_{0}>0
is deduced by applying the above result to
r^{-a}e^{-br}
.

(
4
). Denote
g(r)=r^{a}\left(1+\left|\ln r\right|\right)
. For
r>1
,
